# Current Formal Frontier Toward Proving or Disproving the Riemann Hypothesis

## Research map of HTGeometry, HilbertMetric, and RHTower

**Corpus cutoff:** 15 August 2026, committed snapshot
**80be7c4a7d563db9423e5e4be671cefb7ec84b0e**, plus explicitly identified post-cutoff
shared-tree declarations audited the same day.

This map follows theorem statements, not module placement or research chronology. `Lean-proved`
means an unconditional declaration at the audit tip. Historical `Active-tree Lean` wording in
route paragraphs records live-tree audit provenance; commit `80be7c4a7` contains those
declarations unless a paragraph explicitly says `post-cutoff active-tree`.
Paper-only claims remain labelled.

  Lean proves

  absolute summability under the native shifted-resolvent weight over every Xi divisor copy, and
  premise-free convergence of the full weighted divisor sum to zero. The complete-divisor
  exchange wall is closed. Because this vanishing theorem allows off-line divisors, native weighted
  `J` is a spectrally silent reporter by itself. Active-tree Lean now closes the pre-aggregation
  source identity as well: common-degree padding places the physical even and odd Chebyshev
  channels under one positive Laplace filter and proves
  `a_f^2(C_E-C_O)=pi(2D)! Re sum_q W_f(q)(E(q)^2-O(q)^2)`. Its canonical normalization tends to
  zero, so no oriented boundary anomaly survives. The Nyquist mixed-flux successor is therefore
  terminal. Route U remains open only at the diagonal source obstruction: classify the actual
  common-filter even/detail diagonal capitals for the one fixed cofinal carrier, proving a
  source-specific lower bound or showing any negative diagonal contradicts the degree-free Cauchy
  density. Universal diagonal positivity is RH itself and is retired.
- **L / Garland:** finite weighted-complex Garland localization is complete in Lean, including
  both localization identities, codifferential adjunction, harmonic vanishing, and the
  cohomological theorem that every cocycle is a coboundary under the link-gap hypothesis. The
  symmetric-design incidence identity now yields the actual bipartite graph spectral gap, and the
  Steinberg chain constructs elementary transvections, the homomorphism from Steinberg
  group to invertible matrices, elementary group as its image, and `K_2` as its kernel. This
  advances adjacent tame-kernel/real-Milnor plumbing, but does not touch
  Route L's RH-bearing gap: source-derived singular comparison from completed Green/BV boundary to
  fixed Burnol--Deninger physical landing.
- **Receivers and no-gos:** under RH the Xi Schur germ has an explicit singular atomic circle
  probability measure at Cayley images of real Xi spectral points; this classifies the receiver
  and kills any proposal demanding an absolutely continuous boundary measure. Positive even
  source symmetry alone is also dead as a zero-location mechanism: a positive two-shell source has
  exact off-axis bilateral-Laplace zeros. Schanuel isogeny cores and defect character lattices are
  now classified; rank-one defect slopes descend to the intermediate value field, and a
  nondegenerate integral Möbius transform cannot hide a non-descending slope. Their remaining
  arithmetic specialization problem still has no Xi-typed landing. Lean computes the exact
  Kähler defect under an integral power plus affine period shift: branch change subtracts the
  period differential. It proves a nonzero old defect and a newly adjoined transcendental period
  differential are linearly independent, and a transcendental defect slope makes every nontrivial
  integral pair of period shifts rank two. Lean further proves that intersecting value fields over
  all nonsingular integral two-by-two isogenies gives exactly the same core as intersecting only
  over common positive scalar powers; the larger matrix family cannot hide a smaller arithmetic
  core. Lean also proves scalar powering creates no independent vanishing equation:
  `a^n-b^n` is `(a-b)` times its geometric cofactor, which is nonzero at every nonzero diagonal
  point in characteristic zero, so local zero multiplicity is unchanged. Branch choice therefore
  cannot generically collapse a genuine period direction. Lean also proves a sharp
  stage-two alternative: if `alpha` is nonzero algebraic and a second edge `z,exp(z)` lies in the
  relative algebraic closure of `Qbar(exp(alpha))` while `z` is not a rational multiple of `alpha`,
  then the two-variable Schanuel extraction statement is false. Thus Schanuel forces relative
  exponential-edge rigidity; the corpus neither proves that rigidity nor finds the counterexample.
  This remains an upstream no-hide/classification law with no Xi landing.
  Constitutive finite-formation syntax collapses to affine leaf constraints, and its cofinal work
  is finite only at neutral character; bilateral exponential repair then proves every positive
  self-generated repair stage creates a next reporter unbounded on its current fibre. These are
  sharp formation/properness no-gos, not an Xi-typed producer. Rational unit-shift difference
  rigidity reduces every constant-increment rational reporter to an affine one. Barnes--Mellin now
  controls all polynomially weighted vertical derivatives and arbitrary-order remainders, and the
  half-line kernel `(1+y)^(-c)` has the exact Mellin value
  `Gamma(w)Gamma(c-w)/Gamma(c)` on `0<Re(w)<Re(c)`.
  Asymptotic-law cores are now compact convex tail invariants commuting with continuous affine
  reporters. Strict-convex no-loss forces a singleton source core; three jointly recoverable
  proper strict-convex reporter energies propagate that selection to every fourth affine reporter.
  Static shortcut mass is likewise forced into either same-channel recurrence energy or a growing
  family of occupied observer channels. Lean strengthens this to genuinely distinct
  memories whenever observer targets have positive resolution separation. Lean then
  subtracts the unavoidable diagonal self-memory exactly: under a uniform off-diagonal coherence
  ceiling, bounded channel capacity can support a long history only by paying genuine cross-time
  recurrence inside reused channels; otherwise occupied-channel count must grow. Its active-tree
  unit-sample corollary gives the corresponding quadratic-cardinality versus linear-cardinality
  capacity inequality. These are exact producer templates, but the
  corpus supplies neither an Xi-typed recoverable reporter triple nor the required nonlinear
  no-loss law. Prime-occupation, q-ladder, orbifold-tail, and recurrence theorems therefore enlarge
  infrastructure without instantiating another RH route.

No new independent lettered route appears. T and U remain the latest distinct architectures.
G/CCM and O still share one ground-space problem—control every true restricted-Weil near-ground
direction—but retain different source mechanisms. Latest theorems narrow G, CCM-FU, O, P, T, U, and
adjacent L infrastructure; none proves or disproves RH.

## Abstract

This paper maps every proof architecture toward the Riemann hypothesis currently instantiated by
the corpus. Routes are quotiented by their first unresolved source theorem, not by shared
vocabulary or final RH criterion. Each dossier states endpoint, proved mechanism, obstruction,
open core, and retired subclasses. Lean declarations are authoritative; paper claims are
separated from formal ones.

Neither RH nor its negation is proved. The independent live lettered routes are C1, D2, E, G, L,
O, P, Q, T, and U. The unlettered CCM-FU lane is also live. Routes N and R are terminalized: their
remaining admitted producer premises are RH-equivalent. Routes T and U are new independent
architectures exposed by the enlarged corpus:

- **Route T** converts the mass-shifted Xi divisor geometry into one exact critical outgoing
  current and then into a finite centered von Mangoldt polynomial inside a moving mass light cone.
- **Route U** follows the first loss of positivity in the shifted-Xi Hankel tower and identifies
  its crossing form with a completed Euler--Gamma extension of an adaptive null flag.

These are not renamed versions of existing routes. Route T has a first-order critical
von-Mangoldt flux endpoint, whereas E has squarefree Möbius parity transport and terminalized N
has a prime-normal domain classifier. Route U has an adaptive finite Hankel null-face crossing endpoint,
whereas G seeks a cofinal Suzuki two-angle meromorphic carrier and P seeks one compact positive
moment measure.

No additional live lettered route is instantiated at this cutoff. Seven possible Route-V
architectures now have nontrivial cores: conservative Euler-shell/Witt scale completion,
triptic orbital-divisor normal form, tropical-projective source-normal descent, and relative
formation-completion current, coefficient-faithful Helson Fredholm-wall response, and the
prime-log jump--Sonin Mellin-grade architecture, together with the Gamma-six
Guinand--Weil contour-flow architecture. The first
lacks its literal jointly convergent source inverse pair; the second now has an exact additive and
multiplicative kernel theorem but no coefficient-faithful Xi landing or critical reflection law;
the third has a prime-clock projective-width selector but no Xi-typed observer/descent theorem.
The triptic branch now also has exact Kummer content, maximal root order, quotient-group
classification, and realization of every finite divisor class by elliptic-gamma products. It
still has no Xi landing or reflection orientation. The fourth has an Lean-proved centered-jet/evenization classification, but no literal
arithmetic relative extension or source law annihilating its hidden current.
The fifth has a trace-class coefficient-faithful Helson realization and a nonzero simple-wall
crossing covector, but no canonical Xi coefficient stream, fixed RH threshold, or global wall law.
The sixth has a literal two-prime BV Mellin frame, cofinal Sonin transport, and an exact
critical-grade norm falsifier, but no source-defined Xi-mode dagger/isometry.
The seventh has an exact multiplicity-faithful continued prime--archimedean contour equal to a
finite Gamma-six divisor-flow pencil, and its low-height part is unconditionally discharged; its
high-height subexponential source estimate is still exactly equivalent to RH and has no
independent producer theorem.
The reciprocal-character virial and inverse-response Ward laws give a concrete possible producer
inside the existing C1/CCM/Gauss architecture, not a seventh independent candidate route.
The source-horizontal/Gauss--Hodge weighted-resolvent field is now the sharpest proved fusion of
C1, U, and P language: its coefficient is a literal reflection-doubled source current and its
endpoint is one complete-monotonicity wedge, but the required source-side trace/Markov law is
absent. It therefore narrows existing routes rather than instantiating Route V. The cubic
Ward/Hellinger construction is a further serious opening: P-G174 puts the full arbitrary-cluster
kernel on the primitive Euler carrier with one global summable trace budget, while the zero-side
separator remains uniformly charging. P-G175 proves that global source budget is strictly smaller
than one zero-side quantum. P-G176 proves that imposing the corresponding scalar capacity law on
all strict-right zero packets is exactly RH. P-G178 supplies a coefficient-faithful pre-zero
arithmetic incidence between primitive scales: depth squaring doubles the clock, retains amplitude
with modular gain `sqrt n`, and is an exact `1/8` contraction in cubic source measure despite being
unbounded in counting norm. What remains absent is the completed Gamma--Euler/Xi landing from that
source block to divisors; it must derive capacity rather than assume it. Route P is therefore not
added to the Route-V count.
Formal Green-square analysis also suggests a possible future quotient of P into C1:
both endpoints meet at positive spectrum of the same source-built `B^2`, with all nonpositive bulk
localized to one rank-two boundary current. The present Lean comparison reaches the P endpoints
through RH equivalences and does not construct a direct source-to-measure factorization, so P is
not merged at this cutoff.
The newly formal adelic Plucker-history, interaction-spectrum formation wall, essential-formation
quotient, passive-cycle law, and static-heterogeneity observer also do not instantiate Route V.
They give exact source mechanics, including the prime reflection wall at exponent one and
invariance under summable compensators, but no theorem identifies physical off-center Xi
displacement with their formation parameter or lands their neutral selector in a fixed Xi fibre.

These additions do not instantiate Route V. The dephased Ward--Green exterior construction is a
second Route-P lane because it uses the same completed prime--Gamma/Hedenmalm source family and
asks for the same kind of source-side arithmetic budget; it is not a separate letter. The CCM
operator chain remains G/CCM's localized operator layer, while collision and probe-circle laws
without pre-zero source budgets are receivers. First open source theorems are now: U's
common-filter diagonal classification for the fixed cofinal carrier after its mixed Nyquist flux
closed to zero; G/CCM's complete-ground selection and lower control, with G's separate global
two-ray tempered subtraction/return and physical trace selection; P's high-tail curvature ceiling
or a completed Gamma--Euler/Xi landing carrying P-G178's exact cubic-weighted depth-square
contraction through a two-variable/determinant-line conormal form and deriving, rather than
assuming, the P-G176 terminal capacity law;
T's actual Ingham--Gabriel coefficient-three zero-counting interpolation from its now-closed fourth
moment/detector budgets, followed by prime supply and signed Perron/cell/gap/current composition,
plus pole-preserving Wold continuation; and L's
singular source-to-fixed-physical specialization. Route R's former Calkin/Fisher alternatives are
terminal RH receivers. A current Helson/
Fredholm wall remains a candidate rather than an additional route because it still lacks a canonical Xi
coefficient stream and source-derived global orientation.

Present older-route frontiers are also narrower. Route E has explicit source-predeclared physical
relations, exact source-faithful shell laws, a complete Granville--Harper--Soundararajan reduction
to the Halasz distance term, and two Lean-proved analytic bridges from fixed-width

the apparent square-multiple polynomial is identically zero for Möbius. Every fixed frequency height,
and in fact a cofinal unbounded
low-height window governed by the `o(M)` Möbius Abel scale, is unconditionally good. The remaining

marker theorem factors its unrestricted finite-prime model, proves antiscale coherence is the exact

`product_p (1+2/p^2)/(1+2/p)^2`, which vanishes with harmonic marker mass despite cofinal

Euler gauge contractively only on strict-right inward Hardy nodes; the Mellin half-line is outward
and its raw Möbius scalar observer is not in `l2`. Missing object is therefore a genuinely finite

The strict-right obstruction is now Lean-proved: exact half-line node equation,

Its exact graph form is weighted projection distance
`d_E^2 <= X^(-1+o(1))`; equivalently every target reporter satisfies normalized

bounds signed Gibbs current by isolated mass, co-moving term `(H/X)Z`, and reciprocal-degree

further to subpolynomial edge Dirichlet energy of inverse incidence load
`q=2/(row+column)`, plus weighted-isolated mass; no Metropolis support overlap is needed.

parity-agnostic outgoing rows on both signs. Existing reciprocal formula is parity-agnostic but

physical graph.
Complete-bipartite block saturation proves exponent-level
row/co-degree estimates cannot supply it, while formal friable-isolation theorem fences literal
bounded one/two/four-prime replacement graph; failed-route density audit upgrades this to linear

Published averaged Chowla itself is not formalized. Relation formation and maximal iteration are

The finite layer makes three formulations exact without closing their arithmetic producer:

inverse-load flux is not a fourth producer: Lean reconstructs the original signed current after

Route O
has Lean-closed exact compact and exterior Bessel/prolate primitives, while its RH-bearing gap

class and evaluates its forward and reflected physical characters; simultaneous nonvanishing is

The missing theorem must be source-selected before divisor specialization; bounded multipliers,
corona quotients, and ordinary operator-topology approximation are closed. Route L
additionally has a nuclear prime-log-height Köthe/Calderón source, strict entire bar contraction in
every character sector, a canonical first linearized Sullivan primitive, and a literal
Möbius--von Mangoldt ray connection with unconditional Mertens/Abel convergence. Its concrete
Möbius--Fourier detector, Poisson source, exact kernel
`ker S_mu=Z(H_cap)`, one-Euler-depleted kernel transport, Meyer seminorm topology, and continuous
moment maps are now Lean. The exact chain-lift obstruction is also classified:
its vanishing, Poisson-range membership, canonical Meyer prelift, and existence of any
Meyer--Poisson lift are equivalent. The scientific gap is the realized two-prime obstruction's
signed relative chain descent and fixed-reader residue cancellation. Paper-level Gamma-six
analysis now proves the forward face is the only vanishing-source physical `L2` singular
transport, its Xi landing has an unavoidable nonzero Gamma residue, and the functional equation
supplies a character-sensitive opposite pole germ. Honest pre-normal source also has infinitely
many tame/Kummer-null but rational-Witt-visible cyclotomic denominator places. Their two normal
orientations have the same common-pole residue, so this cofinal layer is reflection-even and its
tensor-square grade gives a nuclear mixed-`L1/L2` admission which splits off from the odd core.
The actual two-slot odd pole pair also has a source-first nuclear coefficient carrier whose
`L1/L2` character domain is exactly the full open critical strip. The first surviving Möbius
atom forces tensor-square edge \(6^{-m}\); in the corresponding nuclear critical-edge Köthe

boundary formation is therefore closed and direct source-homology descent is a no-go. Missing
shell transport is also classified: the unique forward/reflected carrier acquires Wold factor
`(1/6)^(2 delta)`, so no scalar exceptional coefficient transports every grade off center. Missing

this trace-class subobject and carrying both reflected principal symbols to the completed Xi
Green/Calderón--Witt target, together with signed relative descent and fixed-reader cyclicity. No

parallel handoff in Lean: one fixed compact
theta-source inverse has Hilbert--Carleman determinant vanishing exactly on the Xi divisor, with

inverse-flow trace, fixed-width Xi--Weil polarization, prime--Gamma coefficients, shifted
cokernel/source equation, squared-Cayley growth receiver, and self-adjoint compact-resolvent
supersymmetric Dirac are now Lean-proved. They close operator realization and
certification, not spectral orientation. The direct-Cayley/two-port resolvent extension is now
Lean-proved but remains a receiver. C1's separate eigen-pair explicit-form connector still has one

A now Lean-proved Cassini--Wold/Deninger chain recuts Route T exactly. It
identifies a positive Stieltjes--Cassini orientation defect, its reflected phase-clock and BV--Wold

This is a Route-T recut, not an independent Route V. None of these additions changes route count.

## 1. Reading rules and status vocabulary

**Endpoint** is a proved implication or equivalence whose conclusion is RH. It is a receiver, not
evidence for its premise.

**Open core** is first unproved source statement after all proved connectors are removed. An
assumption equivalent to RH is not called progress.

**Closed mechanism** means corpus proves advertised construction cannot provide required
information. Closure is scoped: it does not refute every future mechanism using similar language.

**Positive survivor** is strongest nontrivial theorem exposed by a failed route: rigidity,
classification, exact boundary, or replacement design constraint.

**Independent route** requires a literal source object, an unconditional chain to an RH endpoint,
and a first unresolved producer not identical under the corpus's source-isogeny comparisons to an
existing route.

The live independent count is ten lettered routes plus CCM-FU. This count is conditional only
on current corpus identification: future comparison could merge routes, and a constructed
post-J principal-relative current would add another.

## 3. Common source architecture and merger verdict

The corpus constructs a source atlas far beyond classical scalar criteria:

1. Wold activation gives zeta ratios and logarithmic derivatives.
2. Squarefree breadth and prime-power depth are separated before scalarization.
3. Möbius depth differences, Fisher damping, primitive von Mangoldt promotion, and Gamma
   whitening are explicit.
4. Regular and singular source-to-Xi landings retain divisor and multiplicity jets.
5. Reflection is tracked before positive energy, determinant, or scalar frequency fusion.

Several apparent routes share one horizontal obstruction. Route R's graded determinant anomaly,
Route L's monodromy/Green obstruction, and the CCM Bockstein are source-isogenous realizations of
one reflection-odd horizontal class. Increasing vector dimension, Kummer depth, or route count
does not raise this atlas rank. The common operation previously missing was:

~~~
SOURCE-ZERO-TO-HORIZONTAL-CARRIER:
construct a source-derived relation which retains and orients the horizontal class before
bounded scalarization, while preserving prime labels, divisor multiplicity, and both reflection
orientations.
~~~

`SourceZeroHorizontalCarrier.lean` closes this operation without bounded scalarization. For every
Xi-divisor multiplicity copy it pairs complete-resolution reflection displacement with the full
ordinary-prime Gamma Bockstein family. Ordered adjacent source ratios recover the exact horizontal
grade; both components reverse under `rho ↦ 1-conj rho`; source-tail log times every prime
component is strictly positive exactly off the critical line. The physical Rees--corona landing
retains prime, depth, divisor, and multiplicity provenance.

This completes carrier construction and orientation, not annihilation. Lean proves global carrier
vanishing equivalent to RH. The pairing of the two retained cofinal orientations is now itself
constructed and signed: weighting the product of the tail-log and prime-Bockstein odd components
by the assembled Gauss residue current gives the Hodge mass current
`H(q,p) = (log 2/2)·current(p)·density(p)·t_q·(p^{t_q} - p^{-t_q})`, `t_q = 2 Re q - 1` —
unconditionally nonnegative, reflection-even, label-faithful, with exact critical radical
(`GaussHodgeMassCurrent.lean`). The Hodge sign of the constructed pairing therefore holds; zero
total mass of this nonnegative current is proved equivalent to RH. A formalized interface
firewall shows the mass-zero law cannot follow from the pairing's own interface even with
divisor-reflection closure: an explicit reflection-closed off-line configuration has positive
mass. The seam-energy bridge (`GaussHodgeSeamEnergyBridge.lean`, with the hyperbolic
comparison layer in `HTGeometry/HyperbolicBounds.lean`) then proves the Hodge mass current and
the Burnol FE--Schwarz seam reflection energy are one invariant: two-sided domination with
explicit constants (`log p/2` and `2√p/log p`; every divisor copy is proved to lie in the open
strip), pointwise co-vanishing, and RH ⟺ prime-2 seam-charge vanishing ⟺ prime-2 Burnol
character unimodularity ⟺ full seam-energy vanishing on the divisor. Because the corpus's seam
layer already evaluates the full prime sum — seam energy at a copy equals the convexity second
difference `M(1+2σ)+M(3−2σ)−2M(2)` of the moment curve `M(β)=Σ_p (log p)² p^{−β}` — the
common open core is now exactly one **horizontal divisor transform**: the divisor average of
that convexity defect. A formalized information-gap theorem fences it: the FE-pair sample of
the prime character is the constant `1` and no function of it computes the Hermitian sample
`‖char‖` — the missing evaluation lies strictly outside the holomorphic sample algebra
(the `Σg²` vs `Σ|g|²` gap as a theorem). Known fencing no-gos: density envelopes cannot decide
the horizontal transform, and its bilinear upgrade lands on diagonal-vs-off-diagonal
separation — the MR/correlation wall. External anchors: Suzuki's screw function
(arXiv:2206.03682) and Connes--Consani Sonin-space Weil positivity (arXiv:2006.13771).
`GaussHodgeCoherenceBudget.lean` then prices the residue exactly: the divisor is
conjugation-closed and `char(ρ)·char(conj ρ) = p^{2Reρ−1}` — the missing transform is the
conjugate-pair diagonal of a squared explicit formula (the pair-correlation classification as
a theorem) — and a Cauchy--Schwarz coherence budget makes the Hodge/seam mass an exact
certificate for zero-side exponential-sum coherence, collapsing to square-root cancellation
`2·|F|` under RH. The successor gate is a limit-exchange statement: vertical time-averaging
extracts the (multiplicity-squared) diagonal for finite families via the completed-time torus
layer; what is open is the interchange of that mean with the divisor closure, priced by
multiplicity squares (zero-simplicity layer) and fenced by the truncate-first co-Poisson
no-go. That interchange is now itself PROVED (`GaussHodgeVerticalExtraction.lean`): for every
ℓ¹ weight and every unimodular mode family — no gap, separation, or correlation input — the
Cesàro mean-square converges to the equal-mode pair diagonal (dominated convergence over
coefficient pairs), instantiated over the full divisor with the vertical flow at the
ordinates; the reflected partner shares the ordinate and the reflection-odd 2×2 block of the
extracted diagonal is exactly the cosh defect. The single unproven step left in the whole
chain is sourcing the ℓ¹ vertical pencil `Σ_ρ v_ρ·char(ρ)·e^{i n Im ρ}` from prime-side data:
the vertical mode is a conjugate-pair (bilinear) sample, and the information-gap theorem
proves no holomorphic channel supplies it. `GaussHodgePencilSourcing.lean` then closes the
loop on that step: the vertical mode has a holomorphic FE companion `e^{ρ−1/2}` whose pencil
IS prime-side data (`char_p·(e^{ρ−1/2})ⁿ = char_{p·eⁿ}` — the zero current at flowed scales),
whose polar factorization exhibits the sourcing defect as the radial mass factor `e^{t/2}`,
and **sourcing ⟺ RH** exactly (`riemannHypothesis_iff_feMode_eq_verticalMode`); under RH
every vertical-pencil value collapses to explicit-formula data. The Gauss/Hodge/seam
programme is now a single closed equivalence loop with every link an unconditional Lean
theorem; its one non-circular entry point is an independent (necessarily bilinear) evaluation
of the vertical pencil. `GaussHodgeWeilPolarizationFusion.lean` fuses the loop with the
corpus's Weil-positivity interface: at the prime exponential test the Weil pair summand of
`xiWeilPolarization` is constantly 1 (blindness, in-interface), the pair-square law holds for
every test over each reflection pair, the sharp-difference square of the prime test is the
cosh defect, hence the H-mass density is exactly the per-pair gap between the Hermitian pair
energy and the Weil polarization value, RH ⟺ that gap closes at prime 2, and the eigen-Gram
of the polarization is unconditionally supported on conjugate pairs with value
multiplicity·XiC′(α)·conj XiC′(conj α). A Gate-II entry — compressed-trace positivity matched
to `xiWeilPolarization` — now lands directly on the H-mass. The remaining content of this entry
is a source-derived global compressed-trace/intersection identity whose positivity controls the
Hermitian pair energy, not merely the blind Weil pair sample.
`GaussHodgeWeilGNSSocket.lean` makes the handoff exact: RH is equivalent to a global coordinate
square realization of every square-summable diagonal Weil value, and such a realization is
equivalent to Hodge-mass vanishing. The converse realization constructed under RH is tautological,
so this is a receiver socket, not a square-realization theorem. The missing producer is an
unconditional source-derived square/GNS or compressed-trace realization on the actual multi-place
class-space object.
`GaussHodgeWeilKreinDecomposition.lean` executes the direct reflection-pair construction and
classifies its exact obstruction. Unconditionally
`4·Re W(F,F) = Σ‖pair sum‖² − Σ‖pair difference‖²` on square-summable tests. The negative
sector's prime-test density is exactly the Hodge/cosh mass, and its annihilation for every test,
the square realization, Hodge-mass vanishing, and RH are equivalent. Thus indefinite Krein
realization is unconditional but not a producer: the direct pair-sum GNS subroute leaves precisely
the RH-bearing negative sector. A Gate-II theorem must annihilate that sector through an
independent arithmetic class-space law, not repackage it.
`GaussHodgeWeilFundamentalSymmetry.lean` names that sector's geometry: the parity flip
`J F = F ∘ conj` is an unconditional fundamental symmetry of the Weil form — J-self-adjoint,
involutive, with J-twisted form equal to the divisor ℓ² inner product — so the GNS Hilbert space
a realization would build already exists as the J-metric ℓ². Divisor parity is W-orthogonal;
the even sector carries an unconditional square realization, the odd sector is exactly minus ℓ²;
the diagonal Weil form is real. RH is the degeneracy law: RH ⟺ every divisor-odd test vanishes
on the divisor ⟺ W equals its own J-twist. The producer target is thereby minimal: any exogenous
positivity that forces J-twist invariance of W closes RH; nothing weaker than killing the odd
ℓ² sector suffices.
`GaussHodgeWeilEntireDetection.lean` minimizes the producer's burden: a multiplicity-exact
entire witness (normalized terminal divided quotients at an off-line point and its reflection)
shows the rough test class adds nothing — RH ⟺ Weil positivity on entire square-summable tests
alone, ⟺ entire divisor-odd tests vanish on the divisor, ⟺ parity-twist invariance on entire
tests. Any producer controlling the analytic class (explicit formula, compressed trace, CC
compression) therefore controls everything; no positivity on non-analytic tests is ever needed.
`GaussHodgeWeilDeviationMoment.lean` and `GaussHodgeWeilSymmetryInsufficiency.lean` close the
producer question from both sides. The deviation moment identity factors the negative sector
exactly: Re W(F,F) = Σ‖even‖² − Σ (β−1/2)²·‖ΔF‖² (deviation × divided difference), with the
unconditional strip bound Re W ≥ Σ‖even‖² − (1/4)Σ‖ΔF‖², and RH ⟺ the deviation moment
vanishes. The insufficiency theorem proves the source cannot be symmetry: an explicit even,
conjugation-real entire comparison function with strip-placed off-line zeros carries an entire
test of negative Weil diagonal, so any producer must consume the Euler product. The open core
in final form: an arithmetic inequality dominating the deviation-weighted divided-difference
moment by the even energy on entire tests.
`GaussHodgeWeilArithmeticDeviationGap.lean` makes the Euler product act on the Weil form for
the first time: PNTPlus's zero-free region (3-4-1 positivity) plus the functional equation bound
every zero's squared deviation strictly below 1/4 — by a uniform arithmetic gap at height ≤ 3
and by the de la Vallée-Poussin log⁹ margin above — improving the deviation-moment lower bound
to Re W ≥ Σ‖even‖² − (1/4 − δ)Σ‖ΔF‖² on low-supported tests. Calibration: known arithmetic
positivity repels zeros from the strip walls (weight < 1/4); the socket needs the weight at 0.
That remaining distance is the lane's exact open problem.
`GaussHodgeWeilTotalDeviationMass.lean` compresses the wall to one number: the total
normalized deviation mass Σ'(Im α_q)²/‖α_q‖² converges unconditionally (genus-one Hadamard +
strip), and RH ⟺ it equals zero. The all-heights arithmetic ceiling (uniform gap below height
3, log⁹ margin above) bounds every squared deviation strictly below 1/4 and yields the
unrestricted weighted deviation-moment bound for the Weil form and the ceiling-weighted cap on
the mass. The lane's entire content is now: known mathematics traps the mass in [0, ceiling·Hadamard];
RH is the statement that it sits at the left endpoint. This theorem family is committed and
Lean-proved; it sharpens the endpoint but does not supply the arithmetic law forcing the
mass to vanish.
`GaussPositiveCubicCriticalMass.lean` closes a tempting trace substitute negatively. The literal
folded Gauss block is a positive operator; positive-moment log-convexity amplifies its retained
first Fourier coefficient into the cubic diagonal. For three components, both reflected
orientations eventually dominate a fixed fraction of the critical sampled energy, every cubic
diagonal term is nonnegative, and the partial sums diverge to `+infinity`. Thus an unrenormalized
positive cubic trace cannot be the missing finite annihilator. Any global Gauss trace law must be
signed, relatively renormalized, or cancel through a distinct class-space differential.
`GaussReciprocalCharacterAdmission.lean` supplies a second exact normalization: the Hodge mass is
the virial derivative of one reciprocal-character split energy, has a quadratic centered-rate
lower bound, and its all-prime family is summable exactly at centered rate zero. Universal
inverse-response Ward mechanics shows what could force such a positive virial current to vanish:
a common inverse formation whose mixed source acceleration is balanced by its boundary anomaly.
The strict invariant-trace specialization is grade-blind, so any viable Gauss producer must be an
anomalous/source-relative Ward identity, not an ordinary trace. No theorem constructs this
arithmetic inverse formation or proves the required compressed-trace admission; the summability
condition itself is exactly RH. This is a sharpened C1/CCM/Gauss entry mechanism, not Route V.

`GaussHodgeWeilPositivityCone.lean` now gives the maximal unconditional positive region currently
visible from the certified height floor and real-ray resolvent budget. If an entire square-
summable test's tail deviation is dominated by resolvent mass with constant `C`, its Weil diagonal
is nonnegative once even energy pays the explicit ray-jet budget; at the critical probe the
coefficient is `396/6475`. This is useful local coercivity, but the same module proves the cone
annihilates detector vectors: if even energy is zero, cone admission forces the complete deviation
moment zero. Off-line odd witnesses therefore cannot be bootstrapped into this unconditional cone.

`XiProbeCircleCriterion.lean` identifies the complementary information wall. Every divisor copy's
budget response at probe `c` is the average of the on-line profile at probes displaced by its
horizontal deviation. In pole coordinate `x=(c-i conjugate(alpha))^-1`, line placement is exactly
the Hermitian circle law `Re x=c|x|²`; requiring it for all divisor copies at any one real probe is
equivalent to RH. Holomorphic ray moments and finite probe samples do not determine this quadratic
Hermitian relation. The C1 producer must therefore be a source-derived Hermitian/intersection law,
not additional ray-jet bounds, probe sampling, or enlargement of an unconditional positive cone.

The coefficient-faithful weighted edge chain now gives a sharper joint C1/U/P receiver. For any
summable discrete complex spectrum with positive coefficients, nonnegative real parts of all
weighted power moments force a positive-real atom of maximal modulus. Applied to the exact bounded
nonlinear Wold polar coefficient, this proves at every `X>=0` that positivity of all full-divisor
weighted resolvent moments is equivalent to RH. The stronger Gauss--Hodge specialization needs no
bounded normalization: for every fixed Gauss depth and prime label, the literal reflection-doubled
mass current is nonnegative, multiplicity- and label-faithful, zero exactly on line, uniformly
bounded over the divisor, and an admissible coefficient for every resolvent power.

Those moments are the derivative jet of one real source-resolvent field. At any `X>0`, all orders
`n<=18` and the far sector `(n+1)^2<=X` are unconditionally nonnegative; under false RH they are
strictly positive. Every negative witness forced by weighted edge detection therefore lies in the
exact complementary wedge

~~~
n >= 19,    0 < X < (n+1)^2.
~~~

RH is equivalent to complete monotonicity restricted to this wedge at one positive shift. This is
not a proof: the current still weights already formed Xi divisor copies. The first source theorem
is a reflection-doubled prime--Gamma trace, Stieltjes, or Markov identity which constructs the same
field before zero selection and proves its wedge derivatives nonnegative. Single-holomorphic-
profile insertion is formally impossible, and defining the measure by the divisor sum is circular.
Thus nonlinear Wold functional calculus, hard carrier selection, separate moment constructions,
low orders, and far-ray estimates are no longer frontier.

Route L asks for the common horizontal annihilator through the resolved-Green
rational-Witt/Milnor source annihilator; Route R asks for it through singular Cartier/Stokes
non-amplification. These target realizations remain distinct even though they retain the same
horizontal class.

The new longitudinal--transverse conservation law explains why this common atlas does not itself
select. Normalized source resolution lives in the zero-codiagonal radical; every intervention that
preserves that radical leaves the divisor coordinate unchanged except for a predeclared scalar
action. A merged architecture therefore needs an independently source-generated nonzero mixing
defect before physical landing. Nonzero mixing is only necessary: it must still preserve labels,
multiplicity, reflection, and the route-specific admission law.

The residue-promotion anomaly makes this constraint sharper. Attaching a new law cell preserves
the existing residue exactly iff the attachment already annihilates the defect. Any compensator
has rank at least the anomaly rank; a fitted rank-minimal compensator splits off as a contractible
summand and creates no new law. Coordinate-stable compensators are infinite-dimensional, no
holomorphic compensator can reproduce the physical mixed defect before divisor specialization,
and the pre-reflection charge has separation rank exactly two. Thus a viable R/L/CCM comparison
must be genuinely mixed and source-relative before specialization; finite rank-one or holomorphic
repairs only re-present or erase the obstruction.

`SoninCofinalReporterClockCommutatorSeam.lean` supplies one exact cross-route splice. On a common
`ell2` source, positive Sonin pullbacks converge strongly to the identity; the transported complex
Fisher score is reporter term plus an explicit clock commutator, and that commutator vanishes on
each fixed source in the cofinal limit. A diagonal sequence lands exactly on Route E's literal
prime-labelled one-corner codiagonal, with common limit `2u/(1+u)^2`. For every `u>0` both sides
are eventually strictly positive. This is a real Sonin/E source identification and a negative
design law: after Fisher scalarization it cannot annihilate an off-neutral orientation. Any useful
splice must retain a reflection-odd or otherwise signed coordinate before this landing. Lean-proved `SoninCofinalComplexPhaseOddFisherSplice.lean` constructs that upstream coordinate. Along
every inward complex ray it proves the exact holomorphic critical Fisher profile
`2u/(1+u)^2`, retains the unitary logarithmic phase before real scalarization, and on one common
cofinal finite schedule subtracts the `+it` and `-it` orientations. The limit is the explicit odd
profile `4it(1-t^2)/(1+t^2)^2`, which vanishes exactly at the neutral class for `|t|<1`. This is a
genuine source-orientation producer at Sonin/squarefree level. It does not identify that odd score
with the completed Xi physical metric or prove an RH-annihilating global law, so it remains cross-
route infrastructure rather than a new proof architecture.

This does not merge C1, D2, E, G, L, O, P, Q, T, and U into one serial super-route. Routes N and R
are terminalized and contribute receiver/diagnostic invariants, not independent producers. The live
routes have different admission and producer theorems. R/L/CCM comparisons cannot be counted as
independent evidence merely because they use different target geometries.

The remaining problems are therefore not mostly missing connector lemmas. They are source
selection, source-relative coercivity, singular boundary annihilation/positive closure, or
genuinely oscillatory all-prime cancellation before information-losing completion.

### Pole-purchase boundary [FORMAL CLASSIFICATION — NOT A ROUTE]

`PolePurchaseCollisionGap.lean` proves the exact limit of modulus-only source estimates. The
Liouville and constant completely multiplicative sequences have identical coefficient moduli, yet

~~~
(sigma-1)|zeta(sigma)| -> 1,
(sigma-1)|sum_n lambda(n)n^(-sigma)| -> 0
~~~

as `sigma->1+`. Thus no modulus-only majorant can certify their real cancellation gap, while one
analytic invariant beyond coefficient size—the zeta pole—does certify it. The same module records
the de la Vallée Poussin nonnegative trigonometric purchase
`3+4 cos(theta)+cos(2 theta)=2(1+cos(theta))^2` and the resulting nonvanishing on `Re(s)=1`.
This is a boundary law for E/T-style arithmetic producers: positive or coefficient-free caps
cannot create cancellation; a viable source estimate must retain a pole, signed phase, incidence,
or an equally informative analytic invariant. It supplies no critical-strip tail estimate and is
not an RH route.

### Cubic Ward/Hellinger source-trace opening [FORMAL — NOT YET A ROUTE]

The committed Ward cluster supplies a genuinely new possible architecture, but not an RH route at
this cutoff. Functional-equation absorption bounds finite strict-right height-window packets by a
source-only pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt quantity. The scale-invariant multiscale
Hellinger--Cauchy separator has positive mixed entries and constant self-charge `log(9/8)/h`, but
its literal balanced Euler diagonal diverges. Cubic log-moment weighting repairs that mismatch:
the zero-side kernel is positive, has self-charge `115/(864 a^3)` and therefore a uniform strict-
right quantum, while the matching balanced prime-depth diagonal
`115/(864 (e+1)^3 p^(e+1)(log p)^2)` is summable.

Exact Bohr dephasing removes distinct-ordinate cubic cross terms and yields a multiplicity-faithful
equal-ordinate energy; a source budget of `N/4` would force at least three quarters of the census
onto the line. P-G173/P-G174 strengthen this to arbitrary collision clusters: the full collision
matrix is PSD, and after resolved-von-Mangoldt congruence on distinct primitive Euler cells its
trace is bounded by one global summable constant independent of frequencies and packet size. Lean
proves more in P-G175: this complete source trace is strictly below `115/108`, while every
strict-right zero has cubic self-charge at least `115/108`. Thus any completed trace contraction
would exclude the first off-line zero rather than merely bound its density. Lean
also proves why this is not yet source-faithful to zeros: genuine holomorphic height translation
has modulus `exp(-tau a)`, and no scalar independent of horizontal depth can turn depths one and
two into the unitary ordinate phases required by Bohr dephasing.

The missing mechanism is a completed coefficient-faithful pre-divisor incidence/contraction from
the now-literal primitive Euler collision carrier to the cubic zero-side energy, with signed
ordinate control retained.
It would now give RH directly by the strict one-quantum capacity gap. This is a serious cross-route
opening in the R/CCM/Ward atlas, not a twelfth route, because the source-to-zero comparison itself
has not been constructed.

P-G180--P-G182 and the subsequent Ward audit sharpen this opening. The dyadic collision layer is
Calderon-reconstructive, so the receiver reduces to one base Cauchy cube with primitive budget
strictly below `2`; that cube is exactly the radial Gram of completed-Xi logarithmic-conormal
residues. Exact divisor normalization would require the nonadditive amplitude `1/sqrt(m)` at a
multiplicity-`m` zero, but RH exclusion does not: the raw residue costs `m^2/(4a^3)>=2`, already
more than the entire primitive budget. Lean proves a bilateral modular landing: the conormal at
`rho` on the positive half-line and the oriented conormal at `1-conj(rho)` on the negative
half-line pull back to a conjugate pair, and their source-reflection pairing is exactly
`1/(4a^3)`. Lean also proves no additive scalar residue law can implement square-root
multiplicity. Thus the minimal remaining theorem is strictly weaker and more source-faithful: a
two-sided pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt raw-conormal Riesz/exterior trace bound below `2`, constructed
and bounded before residue expansion. Critical-feature Fourier inversion cannot supply it because
its exact source profile contains `exp(-|x|)` and removing that factor is unbounded; reciprocal FE
chambers must remain paired through the trace.

### Deninger divisor-defect traces [FORMAL RECEIVERS — NOT A NEW ROUTE]

`DeningerDivisorDefectTrace.lean` constructs the absolutely convergent cubic resolvent trace

~~~
Delta(z)=sum_q (mu_q-conj(mu_q))(mu_q-z)^(-3)
~~~

and proves `Delta` vanishes identically exactly under RH. The third power is the first absolutely
summable twist against the corpus's inverse-square divisor budget. The three-point successor
proves an analogous simple-pole detector and, on the Euler half-plane, rewrites the mixed twisted
resolvent trace as the honest pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt source combination minus that defect. The
three-point defect vanishes for every base triple exactly under RH.

These theorems make the horizontal Deninger/Ward obstruction analytic and prime-comparable, but
both defects are defined after divisor formation. No unconditional source law annihilates them.
They therefore strengthen Route L/R diagnostics and receivers without creating another producer
or changing route count.

## 4. Route dossiers

### Route A — literal CCM first clock [TERMINALIZED]

**Endpoint.** The completed first-clock scalar is nonnegative exactly under its RH orientation.

**Closed chain.** Fixed source-faithful physical-phase transports are identity. Every nonidentity
transport has an explicit phase-connection defect. Aperture-dependent positive Markov transports
exist exactly when the original source scalar is nonnegative; universal feasibility is RH.

**Verdict.** Route A is closed as an independent source-producing mechanism, not as a proof of RH.
First-clock tail bounds, Bohr restatements, and positive mixers cannot reopen it.

**Positive survivor.** Phase-natural rigidity and barycentric orientation completeness provide
exact tests for any proposed successor.

### CCM-FU — four-unit source-codiagonal incidence [OPEN]

**Endpoint.** A moving Gamma/Sonin/Hodge incidence carrying the continued Xi Ward column with
nonincreasing four-unit energy yields the required Ward orientation.

**Proved chain.** The squarefree Fisher lower corner has exactly four critical energy units.
Critical source converges weakly but not strongly, escapes every fixed compact synthesis, and every
internal Xi jet has a source-defined square-summable physical horizon.

**Source-reflection diagonalization.** `CCMGuinandWeilSourceReflection.lean` proves an exact law for
the finite Guinand--Weil event matrix `Q_omega` on integral Fourier modes:

```text
Q_omega + Q_(1-omega) = 2*Diag_n(cos(2*pi*omega*n)).
```

Hence every off-diagonal source-codiagonal incidence is reflection-odd, while the reflected packet's
zero mode is only twice total event weight. Total weight `2` gives the central four-unit value, but
does not orient any nonzero mode: the half-horizon event has value `-2` at mode one, and its reflected
matrix is formally proved not positive semidefinite. This rules out atomwise reflected-event
positivity and every argument extracting orientation from four-unit central mass alone. Missing
producer must assemble distinct prime, pole, and archimedean events and control the reflection-odd
sector through the source-defined physical horizon before scalarization or passage to the cofinal
limit.

**Square reflection-odd rigidity.** `CCMGuinandWeilReflectionOddRigidity.lean` proves rigidity for
exactly `N` distinct reflection orbits observed by the first `N` positive modes. Their sine-moment
determinant is the cosine-node Vandermonde times nonzero sine and Chebyshev leading factors, so
vanishing zero edges forces orbitwise reflected weight balance. The determinant-zero equivalence
locates every square degeneracy at a sine endpoint or cosine collision. This does not cover an
overcomplete or continuous completed source.

**Codiagonal observer-dimension transition.**
`CCMGuinandWeilCodiagonalObserverDimension.lean` supplies the missing rectangular audit. With `R`
event orbits and `N` positive modes, the codiagonal observer is injective on distinct nondegenerate
atoms when `R<=N`, but has a nonzero imbalance kernel for every horizon placement when `R>N`.
Groskin's prime packet can be overcomplete and its pole/archimedean sources are continuous, so
cross-event codiagonal cancellation is structurally available. The finite matrix forgets source
provenance above observer dimension; canonical selection must occur in the source-defined physical
horizon before the `2N+1` source quotient, not be reconstructed from four-unit mass or codiagonal
coordinates afterward. Diagonal weighted-cosine coordinates remain and prevent this codiagonal
kernel theorem from being misread as a whole-matrix kernel.

**Full source observer and whole-matrix kernel.**
`CCMGuinandWeilFullSourceObserver.lean` closes that remaining finite atomic distinction. For modes
`0,...,N`, it packages exactly one weighted-horizon coordinate, `N` sine coordinates, and `N`
weighted-cosine coordinates. Lean proves that these `2N+1` coordinates vanish if and only if the
entire finite CCM event-packet matrix vanishes. Consequently, for every horizon placement and every
`R>2N+1`, rank--nullity produces a nonzero signed atomic source whose whole finite event matrix is
zero. Restoring the diagonal therefore raises the unavoidable ambiguity threshold from `N` to
`2N+1`; it does not recover source provenance. This is a theorem about finite signed atomic events,
not yet the literal prime--pole--archimedean measure or its canonical physical-horizon lift.

**Strict-positive source nonselection.**
`CCMGuinandWeilPositiveSourceNonselection.lean` proves that positivity does not remove this fibre.
For every `R>2N+1` and every horizon placement, there are distinct event-weight vectors with every
weight strictly positive, yet with identical full `2N+1` coordinates and identical whole CCM
matrices. Consequently no decoder—even nonlinear—can recover all source weights from the matrix,
including after restricting its domain to the interior of the positive cone. Thus the missing
positive closure cannot be a post-quotient source selector; it must use arithmetic provenance or a
source-native connection unavailable in the finite matrix.

**Continuous source quotient.**
`CCMGuinandWeilContinuousSourceQuotient.lean` constructs the required pre-quotient source space as
the continuous dual of `C([0,1],R)`. Finite Borel measures act by integration, and their reporter is
literally Groskin's weighted-horizon, sine, and weighted-cosine moment vector. Lean proves for every
continuous source functional that this vector vanishes exactly when its entire finite CCM packet
vanishes. Finite atomic functionals specialize exactly—not only up to kernel—to the preceding
atomic observer and event matrix. Hence continuous pole/archimedean sources no longer sit outside
the formal quotient theory. What remains is arithmetic: instantiate the literal prime, pole, and
archimedean functionals and construct their canonical all-jet transport to the Xi Ward column.

**Open core.** Literal finite-stage theta core and its unique source-faithful whole-`L2` completion
now exist. The finite simple-ground real-zero implication is also parity-free: odd ground no longer
requires an even-ground reduction. The fixed-support analytic passage from mean-square source
convergence to locally uniform compact-Fourier convergence and preservation of real-zero support is
also closed. Construct canonical `Pi(lambda,k)`, prove its explicit simple-ground/rank-two block
hypotheses cofinally, produce the required fixed-support arithmetic mean-square approximation with
nonzero limit, and construct a noncompact family joining the
four-unit corner and physical jet horizon to the continued Xi Ward column and complete ground
selector while preserving source provenance and four-unit orientation. At event-dictionary level,

source dual and construct its canonical source-horizon lift before quotienting. Prove that the lift's
weighted-cosine/connection defect lands in the continued Xi Ward column and obeys the four-unit
bound. Uniform sine-moment conditioning is relevant only on undercomplete subfamilies; it cannot
select an overcomplete or continuous source representative.

**Dead subclasses.** Fixed compact operators, abstract RKHS realizations, and postlimit
codiagonalization are formally inadequate.

**Parity-free simple-ground theorem.** `CCMParityFreeOddGroundFourier.lean` supplies the odd half

It does not prove that the canonical arithmetic `Pi(lambda,k)` has a simple ground, satisfies the

**Fixed-support compact-Fourier limit theorem.** `CCMCompactFourierMeanSquareZeroTransfer.lean` proves the analytic passage needed after the finite
real-zero theorem. On a fixed interval, it bounds the compact-Fourier difference by the square root

This weld also identifies orientation rather than assuming it. The two undressed half-steps have

with no spurious point spectrum. At every determinant zero its analytic order equals the

RH  iff  Re xiResolventHankelForm(N,a,X) >= 0

Every resolvent moment is unconditionally positive once `X>=(n+1)^2`; every Hankel form with

must have both coefficient signs. Its possible negativity is bounded by one `ell2` imaginary mass

constructs finite-dimensional Riesz/Jordan root-block calculus for every nonzero source spectral

torus point, and finite-height quadratic mass is at most

(2W+4 R_Q(1+|Q| log 2))
  * product_(p in Q) (1+p^(-2r))/(1+p^(-r))^2.
~~~

The product is bounded by `exp(-(1/2) sum_(p in Q)p^(-r))`, so it vanishes exactly throughout
critical range `0<r<=1`, despite cofinal modulus-one recurrence.

This one-sided critical classification is now unconditional Lean in
`RouteECriticalAntiscaleDissipationSaturation`. Formal proof includes exact all-order squarefree
frequency expansion, unique-factorization spacing `1/R_Q`, exact coefficient energy `V_(Q,r)`,

State-dependent cofactor transfer has also been recut. For any finite probability weights `w_s`,

sum_(s!=q) w_s w_q (A_s+A_q)d_s

and collision has norm at most `2 w_max ||A||_(L2(w))`. Reciprocal ternary states have

This reduction is reinforced by newly relevant Lean. The general infinite joined
one-corner theorem and its squarefree Möbius specialization give

|hsInner(sourceCorner, contractiveReporter)|^2
  <= (1/2) hsNormSq(sourceCorner),

`RouteEFinitePhysicalCodiagonalGaugeCollapse.lean` now closes stronger composed geometry. When

For every all-order finite squarefree Euler history packet this improves raw gauge-collapse from

Separately, critical lower-corner energy is at most `4(1-exp(-u))` and has density four at `u=0`.

so even the limiting profile has only `2 sqrt(log ell/ell)(1+o(1))` scale there. Dividing out the
Euler gauge makes the finite one-corner norm grow like `ell`; shrinking the tilt cannot create

an outward parameter and the scalar observer has divergent `l2` norm. Both assertions, including

the finite sharp Ramaré/unit-shell centered reporter with a strict-half physical corner. Revised
analytic/physical target is
`FINITE-PHYSICAL-COLUMN-ANOVA-STRICT-HALF-AND-SIGNED-RECURRENCE-SEAM`, not statewise flatness or
universal Fisher cutoff tuning. For literal pushed/target column masses `rho,nu`, the exact identity

~~~
chiSquare(rho|nu)
  = N * Var_(nu/N)(rho/nu) + (sum rho - sum nu)^2/N
~~~

is now an unconditional Lean theorem for arbitrary finite unequal masses, including empty
index type without a nonzero-total premise. It shows centered codiagonal energy is precisely
non-scalar column ANOVA. At `N=X^(1+o(1))`, its
endpoint target is `N*Var <= X^(o(1))`. Cauchy--Schwarz then puts centered score and atomic
codiagonal correction at unnormalized size `X^(1/2+o(1))`; maximum-state diffuseness is no longer

For normalized physical laws `alpha,beta` on relation `E`, minimizing this energy over
supported row kernels gives exactly `(A^2/B)d_E^2`, where `d_E` is target's weighted

d_E = sup_f [sum_s alpha_s min_(t~s) f_t - E_beta f]_+
            / sqrt(Var_beta f).

Thus endpoint target is exact all-reporter expansion at X^(-1/2+o(1)). Exact normalized

d_E^2<=epsilon, sharply, so generic 1+o(1) unit Carleson is not rate-sufficient. A target

(g-b)^2/(b(1-b)). Seam cannot be optimized away and restored later.

This projection-duality and strict-half rate firewall is dependency-closed and formalized in

The same Lean-proved module supplies the sharp cut obstruction. If a source subset has

Lean-checked layer-cake strengthening completes finite theorem: normalized weighted Hall is

A generalized-prime firewall now fixes what arithmetic specificity any successor must use. For an

`RouteEComponentHistoryShuffle.lean` makes the component-histogram proposal

Active-tree `RouteEGibbsTerminalChamber.lean` proves that parity cancellation fails maximally on a

- the generic determinant-only converse printed as Theorem 1.8 is invalid: Lean constructs an

- the first unresolved gate is earlier than paired spectral occupancy.  On the half interval write

  `M_0(0)/M_0(1)`, with a canonical near-zero fallback. Fixed-anchor pointed convergence forces
  the selected `c_a->0` and automatically gives the hard-edge law and movable target;
- fixed base does not restore a regular vector limit. For every bounded exponential base
  `c_a->c_*>=0`, including Suzuki's original `c_*=1`, the finite source norms diverge and normalized
  vectors converge weakly to zero in the global Weil embedding. At positive `c_*`, a hypothetical
  Riesz limit would carry the nonunit translation character `e^(c_*t)` under a unitary group; at
  zero it would create a forbidden fixed vector;
- the fixed target is source-declared: `Psi_T=-int_0^T e^(-t)pi^(-1/2)mathfrak S_tdt` is
  unconditional, and under RH converges exponentially to the global base-`i` model kernel with
  probability `M_0(1)^(-1)sum_q(omega_q+1)^(-1)delta_(omega_q)`. Thus the remaining theorem has
  bounded exact form
  `<g_(1,a),(L_a^2+1)(L_a^2+s)^(-1)g_(1,a)>->M_0(s)/M_0(1)` on one positive uniqueness set;
- that singular pointed law has an exact two-extension eigenvalue-only form. If `p_(a,k)` and
  `q_(a,k)` are the positive interlacing eigenvalues of the canonical even and odd extensions,
  compactify by `x(t)=(1+t^2)^(-1)` and put Lebesgue measure `sigma_a` on the disjoint intervals
  `(x(q_(a,k)),x(p_(a,k)))`. Then
  `log(m_a(i sqrt(s))/(i sqrt(s)))=(1-s)int[1+(s-1)x]^(-1)dsigma_a`. Hence the fixed law is
  equivalent to weak convergence of `sigma_a` to the theta/Xi occupancy measure. Spectral
  pollution is not tracked pointwise: nearly cancelling pole--zero pairs contribute only their
  compactified gap length;
- equivalently, for every `j>=0`, the paired smoothing trace
  `Tr((1+(L_a^-)^2)^(-j-1)-(1+(L_a^+)^2)^(-j-1))` must converge to the source-declared logarithmic
  moment of `M_0` at `1`. The zeroth moment is `(1-kappa_a)/2`. On every sufficiently large
  source-visible simple-even-ground aperture, continuity from `kappa_free=2a/sinh(2a)` to `1`
  gives a zero-location-free shift matching the unconditional Xi value `kappa_Xi`; higher trace
  moments and source-visible odd-ground apertures remain open;
- the target occupancy has the unconditional quantitative support wall `x<4/85`. Thus all paired
  gap length on `[4/85+epsilon,1]` must vanish. Finite occupancy is the square wave of the boundary
  phase. Any locally high-frequency regime whose inverse phase speed becomes flat in
  `L^infinity+BV` homogenizes to `(1/2)dx`, leaving the fixed wrong mass `81/170` above the target
  support. Every regular high-clock and smoothly distorted-clock realization is therefore dead;
  the survivor requires alternating phase-speed boundary layers on a fixed spectral window.

`SuzukiMovableAnchorClarkCoherence.lean` fixes the movable-anchor local

source-coherence floor bounds mode count. This is a sharp fragmentation constraint, not the
cofinal pointed-law producer.

`SuzukiTwoPortProjectiveWedge.lean` gives the complementary projective
mechanism. Common ground multipliers cancel projectively: the transverse wedge is

The positive survivor is now one necessarily singular theorem, not two. It no longer needs to be

Formal `SuzukiCanonicalHankelOperator.lean`, `SuzukiPositiveHalfLine.lean`, and the

The same Lean dependency chain now proves Suzuki (2012), Lemmas 4.3--4.6. Compact output is

The new Suzuki carrier audit closes several formerly ambiguous analytic interfaces. For an even

The physical inverse-log grade hierarchy is nuclear in `L2(dL)` for every finite oriented visible

The inhabited dyadic shift-cone obstruction is unconditional on its named edge data, but those data

The Lean-proved Abel stack culminates in
`SuzukiFinitePartLogFourier.lean`. On the literal restricted-Weil core it proves that Abel-damped

These are exact receivers/equivalences, not source producers. Operator construction, spectral/
literal identity, positive-half-line transport, finite compression, and 2012 Lemmas 4.1--4.6 are

The exact edge/source-alignment slack and sharp-edge multiplicity law are dependency-closed in the
committed paper `suzuki-coalescing-anchor-parity-locking-reduction`; they receive no Lean credit.
Dependency-closed paper results classify the geometry of the surviving negative complete-family schedule.
For any successful family with `lambda=-r`, both normalized literal-source spectral laws converge
weakly to `delta_0`, while more than `73/81` of odd probability is forced into the shrinking
rate-free annulus `(r,epsilon_a)` for some diagonal `epsilon_a->0`. The entire cost
`log R_Xi>log(81/4)` condenses into every free probe interval `(r,epsilon)`, forcing an
inverse-width even--odd resolvent-hazard peak without an aperture rate. Exact paired
Rayleigh energy nevertheless stays nonzero: almost all probability collapses to zero while its
energy escapes to spectral infinity through vanishing mass, so the combined second moment
diverges. These paper theorems close uniform-integrability and finite-moment attacks in the hoped-
for direction; they do not close Route G. No Lean declaration is claimed.

**Floor-branch internal reduction.** One route to complete ground-family control first settles the
literal source-admission alternative.
A cofinal uniform bound

~~~
inf spec(C_(a,lambda)^(-1/2) H_a C_(a,lambda)^(-1/2))
  > -77/85+eta
~~~

over every admissible shift kills Route G at its first jet, but it is deliberately stronger than
necessary.  Any strict even floor already violates it near the floor, including a source-invisible
one.  Exact retirement instead requires both:

1. exclude the cofinal source-visible ground-residue reflection cone, including mixed tied floors;
2. prove `E_a(lambda)/O_a(lambda)<=R_Xi` at every remaining finite admissible shift.

On the forced vanishing-shift branch, the remaining theorem must couple condensed infrared parity
transport to ultraviolet energy escape through the literal relative prime--Gamma Hankel geometry.
Weak hard-edge convergence, one-channel Stieltjes bounds, the exact first-moment budget, and any
finite moment hierarchy are now paper-classified as nonterminal.

For the second branch, the normalized-Hankel budget is the sharp operator obstruction.  A surviving
shift must construct source-aligned near-edge vectors; in explicit half-line form their finite
von-Mangoldt reflection correlation exceeds the archimedean Hankel correlation by
`77/85-o(1)` times the common Toeplitz energy, and their same-profile odd/even shifted-form ratio
approaches `R_Xi>81/4`. Raw infinite positive/negative index does not imply this normalized
estimate.  More sharply, the exact narrow packets proving that inertia have normalized quotient
tending to zero; repeating or refining an isolated prime-seam construction is a closed class.

The delocalized branch is now the centered boundary problem.  On every fixed endpoint window the
prime and archimedean Hankel pieces cancel strongly but not in operator norm.  The remaining no-go
is not an unweighted PNT estimate: it must prove that high-frequency atomic recurrences are
dominated by the common finite-part Cauchy/logarithmic energy, uniformly over every source-visible
finite shift.  Equivalently, prove a relative upper bound below `77/85` for the centered
prime--Laplace boundary compression after normalization by `C_(a,lambda)`.  The exact
prime-reflection Dirichlet reserve and log-ratio translation budget become available if this
relative bound forces the reserve small.  No such uniform relative estimate is currently proved.

Shift scale is now class-complete on the negative side for a full family.  Uniformly safe
`-lambda asymp e^a` families are source-blind; the RH conclusion of full identification removes
macroscopic floor hugging; and the uniform source-Rayleigh/Jensen law removes every slower
negative recession.  Expanding-frame annihilation then removes every negative shift bounded away
from zero, leaving only `max(0,-lambda)->0`.  A former local-zero-counting refinement to
`O(log^4(a)/a)` is retracted: its growing low Gram block had no controlled lower bound.  This does
not settle the isolated first-admission gate under false RH, where an admissible shift may follow
a negative floor, and it does not quantify the zero-mass interface.  The positive survivor is now
coupled and exact.  More than `73/81` of the odd source law lies between the shift and some
rate-free shrinking outer edge, and the whole
`log R_Xi>log(81/4)` even--odd transport cost condenses into every shrinking free-probe interval.
The annulus alone has a sharp abstract one-atom realization; even the exact paired first Rayleigh
moment does not exclude it.  In fact the literal laws force the compensating ultraviolet escape,
so uniform integrability is false and the combined second moment diverges.  That escape is not
arbitrary: after energy size-bias and `2a` scaling it is exactly the inverse-square Xi probability
pushed through zero multiplicity.  Remaining negative-side theorem is a cross-scale identity or
inequality coupling the singular infrared parity transport to this linear-aperture multiplicity
clock through source-aligned small singular values of the growing sinc Gram/relative
prime--Gamma Hankel form.  Every successful finite ridge certificate already needs at least
`c sqrt(a)` active ordinates and height `c sqrt(a)/log a`; remaining object is therefore a
coefficient-penalized many-zero packet, not another macroscopic, finite-shift, bounded-cluster,
counting-only, one-channel, or moment-bounded chart.
At fixed aperture the Xi exponentials are complete, so both individual ridge defects can be made
arbitrarily small.  All schedule freedom is equivalently encoded by the source-defined curve
`Phi_a(q)`; closing the negative first gate means excluding the relative ratio
`Phi_a(q)/q->R_Xi`, not excluding approximation in either channel.
Even every zero tail above a moving finite cutoff is complete when the aperture is frozen.  The
missing theorem must therefore be uniform in the simultaneous `a,T,r` limit and must charge
synthesis coefficients or the equivalent Schur self-energy; qualitative tail pruning is false.
The source tail supplies the autonomous coefficient charge `cT/log T`, which kills pure high
reconstruction at `T=r^(-4)`.  Remaining object is specifically source-aligned low/high
cancellation inside the Schur self-energy.

The exact edge/source slack law leaves three operator regimes: `r=1` along a subsequence, a
nonvanishing overshoot `limsup(r-r_*)>0`, or `r-r_*->0` with both literal exponential-source
spectral laws concentrating at `-r`.  The first reduces to the already named ground-pole branch
only when source-visible; the third makes the common-channel Riesz profiles themselves realize the

Suzuki, rule out source-visible instances in all three regimes plus the mixed ground-residue cone;
constructing one passes only the first gate.

Only after that admission gate is passed: construct on one cofinal aperture family, before scalar
fusion and without divisor-fitted shifts, an adjacent ground-pole or interior tangent branch and
prove weak convergence
of its compactified even/odd spectral-shift occupancy measures to the unique measure characterized
by

~~~
int_[0,1] 1/[1+(s-1)x] dsigma_Xi(x)
  = log(M_0(s)/M_0(1))/(1-s).
~~~

Equivalently prove every paired smoothing-trace limit, or the original fixed-anchor bounded
transform

~~~
<g_(1,a),(L_a^2+1)(L_a^2+s)^(-1)g_(1,a)> -> M_0(s)/M_0(1)
~~~

on one positive uniqueness set. This may require pole--zero cancellation under local rank growth
or sub-resolution projection rotation and cannot proceed by regular vector convergence. One
extension spectrum and the matched zeroth trace moment are insufficient. The source-impedance rule
then selects the coalescing base and completes the movable target automatically. Alternatively,
prove a positive
admissible shift cofinally, which terminates the route immediately by Weil positivity.
Equivalently, after impedance calibration, on one nonempty imaginary interval prove exact
Clark-transform convergence

~~~
int_R (t^2+c_a^2)/(t^2+y^2) dnu_a(t) -> M_0(y^2)/M_0(0),
~~~

or, for the interior chart, vanishing of the equivalent resolvent-position Ward residual

~~~
g_a'(y)+(1/y-M_0(0)/(yM_0(y^2)))g_a(y)-c_a(y) -> 0.
~~~

Source admission still requires the label-sensitive or singular category left alive by the
updated modular-superselection and source-horizon papers. Their regular label-blind no-gos do not
kill Suzuki's scalar two-angle family. Nonadjacent boundary schedules, generic real-axis
anti-fragmentation, clean strong-resolvent/one-stable-mode convergence, and any finite-jet
substitute are no longer live endpoints. Neither is regular phase mixing: source work must now
prove or refute the required low-energy alternating inverse-speed boundary layer.

**Current consolidation.** The committed chain removes remaining Suzuki parity and operator-
formation debt. Lemmas 4.7--4.11, equations (4.14)--(4.19), determinant ratio, normalization

y in D(D^dagger) iff -i P_0 y in ran(V^dagger)
~~~

are Lean-proved as well. The preceding source-admission/occupancy formulations are now

The newest screw declarations remove the last natural-domain ambiguity in that statement.

Dom(D^dagger G_a D)=H_0^1(-a,a).

Finally `SuzukiScrewOperatorAction.lean` evaluates the natural product on that domain by the

The exterior branch is now exact through all ordinary frequencies. Every `C^1` positive-half-line
solution of the homogeneous Gamma transport ODE is uniquely `eta^(-1/2) Q(eta)c`.
`SuzukiForcedGammaVariation.lean` proves variation of constants and that bounded forcing
contributes only `O(eta)`, hence no half-log coefficient. The full positive and negative frequency
rays assemble to

~~~
trace_R(L) = 2*pi*L^(-1/2)*beta(c_+,c_-) + O(L^(-1))
~~~

uniformly in truncation. `beta=0` removes the critical term and makes the terminal trace square

singular Gamma corona quotient is exactly one copy of trace space. Whole-frequency assembly and
corona classification are therefore closed.

The distributional point-support step is now finite and explicit.
`HTGeometry/PointSupportStructure.lean` proves that any tempered distribution annihilating
Schwartz functions vanishing near the origin has a representation

~~~
u(phi)=sum_(j<=N) phi^(j)(0)c_j.
~~~

`HTGeometry/PointSupportPrincipalOperator.lean` computes the coordinate-derivative
action and proves the principal Gamma operator `1+2 eta partial_eta` multiplies `c_j` by `2j+1`;
there is no nonzero point-supported principal kernel. Active
`HTGeometry/PointSupportTriangular.lean` proves the corresponding scalar and operator-valued
lower-triangular systems have only zero coefficients whenever every diagonal map has trivial
kernel. `HTGeometry/PointSupportGammaForm.lean` performs the source-specific substitution:
if `u` is supported at the origin, `g` is smooth temperate with `g(0)=0`, `T` is bounded, and

~~~
u(phi)+2u(eta phi')+i u(g phi)-i T(u(eta phi))=0
~~~

for every Schwartz test, then `u=0`. The full weak Gamma frequency equation therefore has no
nonzero point-supported tempered solution; source-specific triangular derivation is closed.
`HTGeometry/CompactPrimitive.lean` and `HTGeometry/IntervalTestSeparation.lean` supply the interval
regularity engine. `SuzukiGammaDistributionalHalfLine.lean` now completes the source-specific
conjugation: on every compact interval away from zero the weak Gamma solution is
`|eta|^(-1/2)Q(eta)c`; uniqueness patches the local vectors to one `c_+` on the whole positive ray
and one `c_-` on the whole negative ray. The same capstone includes the no-point-support
conclusion. Half-line classification is therefore closed. Missing distributional theorem is global
two-ray tempered assembly: realize the two classified profiles as distributions, subtract them
from the physical source, and prove the residual is supported at the origin and retains the exact
weak equation. Point-support rigidity then kills it. The resulting source must still glue
holomorphically across `0<Im z<1`.

`SuzukiFlatFluxChargeAutomatic.lean` removes another independent premise in the exterior assembly.
Superpolynomial endpoint flatness forces `n F_n -> 0`; the localized flux bound plus remainders
forces terminal charge zero, and no bounded physical endpoint supports a nonzero `1/log` dipole.
The endpoint indicial coefficient can be nonzero only at the critical half grade. This closes
flat-charge and dipole matching, not physical source formation, vector-valued Bochner assembly, or
orientation of the surviving channel.

Active-tree `SuzukiLiteralPairedSourceAssembly.lean` closes the next algebraic source seam without
postulating an interior solution. It proves that prime subtraction reverses valuation direction at
the left root and preserves it at the right root, decomposes each translated sample into its
centered marked descendant plus center value, and constructs a smooth scale-buffered cutoff that is
nonzero exactly when the sampled physical point lies in `(-a,a)`. Its paired primitive identity
places derivatives only in the primitive bracket and leaves event terms at order zero. What remains
is analytic and source-specific: completed forcing, derivative regularity, global tempered
two-ray subtraction, and physical trace orientation.

Active-tree `SuzukiGammaStripBranch.lean` and `SuzukiGammaStripGluing.lean` close homogeneous strip
mechanics. On `|Im z|<1`, `Gamma(1+iz)/Gamma(1-iz)` has a nonvanishing holomorphic square root
normalized at zero. The resulting vector-valued homogeneous solution is holomorphic on
`0<Im z<1`; its negative-axis boundary is the channel with `c_-=-i*c_+`. Lean proves this relation
is necessary and sufficient for gluing and equivalent to vanishing of the half-log endpoint trace.
`HTGeometry/ExpSelfAdjointNormBound.lean` and
`SuzukiGammaStripSquareIntegrable.lean` also prove that self-adjoint strip transport grows only
with the real part of its exponent and that every glued homogeneous solution is square-integrable
on every interior line `0<Im z<1`. The remaining strip problem is therefore neither branch
construction nor interior-line integrability: derive that trace relation for the
completed physical source after global two-ray assembly.

`SuzukiGammaStripCarrierTwoSided.lean`, `SuzukiGammaStripContourPairing.lean`, and
`SuzukiGammaStripFourierWeight.lean` close the homogeneous strip at profile level. The exact Gamma
density has matching two-sided bounds

```text
c (1+|x|)^(-(1+2*sigma))
  <= carrier_sigma(x)^2
  <= C (1+|x|)^(-(1+2*sigma)),
```

contour pairing is height-independent, and one measurable frequency profile `F` represents every
interior line with `exp(2*pi*sigma*L)F in L2` for all `0<sigma<1`. Thus separate line data cannot
hide independent homogeneous channels. `SuzukiGammaWallCutoffCommutator.lean` proves the literal
Gamma wall commutator is uniformly bounded, decays exponentially outside the cutoff collar, and
has finite physical block norm throughout the first-pole strip.

Active-tree `SuzukiGammaForcedStripHardy.lean` composes the two-sided carrier with the sharp
weighted Hardy inequality. For self-adjoint transport it proves that a shifted-line `L2` first
moment of the forcing is sufficient for the variation-of-constants solution to lie in `L2` on
either outward ray. This removes a generic forced-propagation estimate; it does not prove that the
completed physical prime--Gamma source has that first moment, nor does it force the boundary
relation `c_-=-i*c_+`.

Post-cutoff `SuzukiPrimeNormalBinomialSeries.lean` closes the prime-normal cross-section expansion:
for self-adjoint `T` with `||T||<=L`, the central-binomial operator series

```text
sum_k [centralBinom(k)/(4^k(2L)^k)] T^k
```

converges in norm to the functional calculus of `(1-t/(2L))^(-1/2)`, with an explicit uniform tail
bound. Every prime descendant grade survives in the expansion. This removes finite truncation and
path-loss ambiguity; it does not force the physical zero-trace relation.

Post-cutoff active-tree `SuzukiPolynomialCarrierGammaDimensionWall.lean` proves the exact tail law
for polynomially distorted Gamma carriers and the sharp threshold

```text
Integrable(|x|^(2*kappa) GammaCarrier_sigma(x))  iff  kappa < sigma.
```

Combining `d/2<kappa` for lattice square summability with the literal strip restriction
