**Formal full-grade Steinberg normal transgression.** The counterterm's source coefficient
is no longer unspecified.  At every depth `m`, the complementary rational pair

~~~
x_m = 3^m/(2^m+3^m),    y_m = 2^m/(2^m+3^m)
~~~

is an honest Steinberg pair and has ratio `x_m/y_m=(3/2)^m`.  In the rational Kummer group
algebra, let `P_m=([x_m]-1)([y_m]-1)`, let `N[u]=(log u)[u]`, and put

~~~
q_m(T)=T(T-L_m)(2T-L_m)/(a_m b_m (a_m-b_m)),
a_m=log x_m, b_m=log y_m, L_m=a_m+b_m.
~~~

The exact coefficient-only identity is

~~~
[y_m]^-1 q_m(N) P_m = [(3/2)^m]-1.
~~~

Its horizontal character is `(3/2)^(m(2 Re(s)-1))-1`, exactly the full-depth successor
reflection anomaly.  Under the half-density Deninger representation it is
`T_(2->3,m)-I`; on the complete tail `h_m=R_(2,m)+Theta` it satisfies

~~~
(T_(2->3,m)-I) h_m = R_(3,m)-h_m -> -Theta.
~~~

Thus algebraic normal escape, physical reflection anomaly, and the nonclosable analytic
successor edge are one source object.  This is not an ordinary Milnor endomorphism or Hochschild
boundary: the Kummer regulator of the output is `m log(3/2) != 0`, while it kills the entire
Steinberg ideal.  General tangent-K/additive-K background is literature-known; the
depth-complementary selector and its exact identification with the complete Deninger--Möbius
successor are project-specific. This closes the successor-coordinate normal but not the actual
Gamma clock; the direct Gamma-six construction below supersedes it on the critical path. An
artificial cone declaring either class a boundary, or ordinary cyclic descent, is forbidden.

spectral imaginary part. The sign premise is already RH-equivalent.

**Further source closure.** Fixed theta Hilbert fibres cannot simultaneously bound translations and
retain the detector; moving fibres absorb the detector. Canonical `L2(k^-1 dx)` landing fails for
every prime aperture by an exact double-exponential endpoint divergence. Moving horizons instead
split into one universal `k(0)/sqrt(p)` boundary atom plus a summable residual. The canonical
Gamma-sewn Möbius soft source, after its source-generated Fisher half-transport, produces exactly
this `p^-1/2` atom. With positive damping `u`, its marked energy is

~~~
p^(-1-u+Im z)+p^(-1-u-Im z),
~~~

and is summable exactly when `|Im z|<u`. At the sharp horizon `u=|t|`, Route N's positive doublet
and Route R's oriented Gamma--Bockstein are the same prime-harmonic essential class modulo `ell1`.
More sharply, the difference of the two Route N channel energies is its canonical Krein charge
`Q_t(p)=p^(-|t|)(p^(-t)-p^t)/p`. The grade-independent source Fisher gauge
`g(p)=p^2/(p+1)^2`, uniformly bounded with bounded reciprocal, satisfies the exact identity
`R_t(p)=g(p)Q_t(p)`. Hence it induces an automorphism of the fixed all-prime quotient carrying
Route N's marked Krein class exactly to Route R's wall class. Orientation is not missing; positive
scalarization had erased it. The Krein charge is summable exactly at neutral grade `t=0`.

Ambient conormal completion is now excluded. For the Fredholm cokernel vector
`g_z(x)=k(x)exp(-i conjugate(z)x)`, weighted mass is exactly
`normSq(g_z(x))/k(x)=|k(x)sourceChar(2z,x)|`; hence every Xi root conormal already belongs to the
weighted theta source space. Yet for every fixed `0<u<1/2`, the ambient conormal `z=iu` fails the
marked damping-`u` fibre. No total ambient base change exists, even without boundedness or
linearity. Completion must follow localization to divisor-supported Fredholm torsion.

The remaining singularity is now explicit. The Krein charge recovers the positive transverse
modular clock character by

~~~
1 + sign(t) p Q_t(p) = p^(-2|t|).
~~~

Away from neutral grade, `p` is the unique diagonal recovery coefficient. It sends the summable
witness `p^-2` to nonsummable `p^-1`, so it cannot preserve `ell1` or act on the fixed quotient.
After the bounded Fisher gauge, Route R's unique recovery is `p/g(p)=(p+1)^2/p` and has the same
obstruction. Hence “singular base change” is exactly one unbounded prime-clock deconvolution.
On Xi support, scalar Cartier atom survival and Route N Krein summability have the same support;
the scalar derived fibre contracts off line precisely when the fixed-trace obstruction survives.

The positive cocycle is now identified with existing product-sector geometry, not merely compared
by its zero set. At critical horizon the two marked channel masses are `p^-1` and
`p^(-1-2|t|)`; their ratio is exactly `p^(-2|t|)`. Their square-root gap obeys

~~~
H_t(p)=(p^-1/2-p^(-1/2-|t|))^2
      =p Q_t(p)^2/(1+p^-|t|)^2.
~~~

Moreover these amplitudes are literally square roots of the existing critical/twisted Wold
valuation masses, and their product is its cross mass `p^(-1-|t|)`. Hence, for the existing squared
local Wold thermofield Hellinger factor `F_t(p)`,

~~~
1-F_t(p)
 =H_t(p)/(1-p^(-1-|t|))^2
 =[p Q_t(p)^2/(1+p^-|t|)^2]/(1-p^(-1-|t|))^2.
~~~

The full normalized thermofield defect is summable exactly at neutral grade. Thus Route N's
Krein wall, transverse modular cocycle, Kakutani numerator, and Wold thermofield sector are exact
polarizations of one Gamma-sewn doublet. Finite Wold thermofield vectors and finite Boolean
contractions already exist; rebuilding them is not an open theorem.

The source dyadic split localizes this doublet further. Squarefree parity odds equal the geometric
Wold parameter, so

~~~
odds_t(p)/odds_0(p)=p^(-2|t|),
odds_t(p)-odds_0(p)=sign(t)Q_t(p).
~~~

The paired-depth residual carries the squared modular ratio, but its twisted-minus-critical
sequence is summable for every `t`; the parity displacement is summable exactly at `t=0`.
Therefore the fixed-quotient modular class is entirely squarefree and the higher dyadic tower is a
regular correction. The generic Boolean--Wold coupling is no longer missing.

Algebraic conormal attachment is also closed. The source-defined squarefree Mellin reporter is
`Z_sq(s)=zeta(s)/zeta(2s)`. On `1/2<Re(s)<1`, `zeta(2s)^-1` is an analytic nonzero unit, while the
archimedean Xi factor is another analytic nonzero unit. Consequently

~~~
ord_s Z_sq = ord_s zeta = ord_s Xi,
~~~

including every multiplicity. The squarefree reporter already carries the complete strict-right
Xi zero germ. What is not supplied by analytic-unit equality is survival and normality of that germ
in the critical exceptional topology.

The remaining projective identification is now exact. Gamma-sewn grade selects
`z_t(p)=-|t| log p`; on `P^1(F_p)` its marked-to-bulk odds are
`exp(2z_t(p))/p=p^(-1-2|t|)`, exactly the twisted squarefree-parity odds. Neutral grade is the
uniform Weyl-fixed projective source. Foreign producer/reporter leakage satisfies

~~~
L_t(p)=H_t(p)(p-1)/(p+p^(-2|t|)),
(1/3)H_t(p) <= L_t(p) <= H_t(p),
sum_p L_t(p)<infinity iff t=0.
~~~

Thus finite projective realization and the all-prime sector classifier are closed. This also
rules out an unexplained same-sector normality premise: scalar projective/Fock completion deletes
every off-neutral germ by orthogonality, so asserting scalar survival would restate the desired
grade. Missing object is source-derived continuation--record correspondence and a source theorem
that Xi conormals have zero complementary record.

All surviving scalar reporters now collapse to one local fracture

~~~
D_t(p)=1-p^(-2|t|)=-sign(t)pQ_t(p),
H_t(p)=D_t(p)^2/[p(1+p^(-|t|))^2],
L_t(p)=D_t(p)^2(p-1)/[p(1+p^(-|t|))^2(p+p^(-2|t|))],
log(1-D_t(p))/(2log p)=-|t|.
~~~

Hence Krein orientation, Kakutani distance, Wold defect, projective leakage, and Poisson/Kadison
impurity are linear or quadratic shadows of one duality--adjunction mismatch. They cannot be
counted as independent pressures toward RH or used cyclically to prove each other vanishes.

Completion preserves this mismatch by reciprocal transfer. With
`A_t(p)=p^(2|t|)`, the inward Sonin survival is `1-D_t(p)=A_t(p)^(-1)`. Every actual strict-right
zero produces a nonzero co-Poisson conormal ladder whose norm tends to infinity. The two reciprocal
edge images have exact Hermitian trace excess

~~~
M_t(p)=D_t(p)^2/(1-D_t(p))=A_t(p)+A_t(p)^(-1)-2,
||T^+y_(j+1)||^2+||T^-y_(j+1)||^2=(2+M_t(p))||y_j||^2.
~~~

The co-Poisson connection is nevertheless flat on its whole quotient. Thus connection curvature
cannot kill the off-line class; its invariant is nonmetricity. At prime dilation ratio, normalized
Burnol holonomy is the oriented coordinate

~~~
H_t(p)=A_t(p)-A_t(p)^(-1)=D_t(p)(2-D_t(p))/(1-D_t(p)),
H_t(p)^2=M_t(p)(M_t(p)+4).
~~~

Route N's positive fracture and Route R's signed holonomy therefore lie on one hyperbolic mass
shell. Reflection reverses `H` and preserves `M`; their conjunction supplies no independent
inequality.

The upstream source-incidence pivot is now exact. Along every nonconstant affine source line from
`Re sigma>1` meeting a strict-right Xi zero of multiplicity `m`, the squarefree overlap `B` and its
division-free Fisher numerator `F` satisfy

~~~
ord(B)=m,  ord(F)=m-1,
not exists analytic q with F=Bq,
lim (v-u)F(v)/B(v)=2m.
~~~

Thus the former charged-graph divisibility gate is impossible. Its positive survivor is the
conormal residue. Centered scaling identifies it exactly with the exceptional Xi affine-current
residue

~~~
[-(rho-1/2)/2]·[2m]=-(rho-1/2)m,
~~~

whose real part is strictly negative for every strict-right zero. Fisher conormal, source normal
cone, and position-times-multiplicity current are one conserved oriented incidence, not three
pressures.

C28 identifies this current with Route T mass propagation. For reciprocal escape `A>1`, put

~~~
u_A=(A+A^(-1))/2,  h_A=(A-A^(-1))/2.
~~~

Then `sqrt(u_A^2-h_A^2)=1`, Burnol holonomy is `2h_A`, nonmetricity is `2u_A-2`, and the Route T
phase has unique saddle `x_*=-a h_A=-(a/2)H`. C27 affine residue is multiplicity times
`-log(A)/(2log p)`. Thus residue, Sonin escape, holonomy, nonmetricity, and mass saddle are
rapidity/orientation/energy/stationary coordinates of one boost charge.

Adaptive mass widening does not escape this wall. Lean proves the conserved identity
`|Im beta_a| Re beta_a = Re alpha |Im alpha|`, so amplification depends only on
`u/Re beta_a`. Any aperture growing at least linearly with time caps the boost of every fixed
divisor. In particular the co-moving aperture makes the literal prime source tend to zero only
while erasing divergent off-line amplification. Source extinction at that scale is a faithfulness
failure, not an RH estimate; any live mass-current attack must remain in a fixed or sublinear
aperture regime.

The deconvolved same-carrier orientation is now exact. The nowhere-zero Euler--Fisher density is
moved into one fixed source ideal by a quotient equivalence; normalized weights remain positive,
the relative character is `p^(-2t)`, and reflection inverts it. The forward orientation preserves
the fixed carrier exactly for `t>=0`, the reflected orientation exactly for `t<=0`, and both
preserve the same carrier exactly at `t=0`; the two orientation classes agree exactly there. This
proves that Fisher damping cannot hide the wall. It is a sharp central-grade receiver, not an
independent producer.

**Terminal no-go.** For an actual Xi divisor copy `q`, put `t(q)=Re(q)-1/2` and
`D_Xi(q,p)=1-p^(-2|t(q)|)`. C21 proves, for every fixed ordinary prime,

~~~
D_Xi(q,p)=0 iff Re(q)=1/2,
[for all q, D_Xi(q,p)=0] iff RH.
~~~

Every hypothetical strict-right squarefree Xi germ has positive defect. Functional-equation
reflection may exchange a free off-line conormal pair; it does not fix either member. Thus zero
complementary record, same-sector normality, projective multiplicative-domain purity, and
duality--adjunction equality are the already retired internal-reflection rank-noncreation premise,
not a Route N producer. Strongest positive survivor is the C19--C28 exact
projective/fracture/escape/holonomy dictionary: every Route N wall is a trace or orientation
polarization of one conserved reciprocal mismatch, while its Fisher source conormal is the
oriented affine residue/rapidity conserved before that transport.
Live work passes to arithmetic injection of this boost charge before divisor formation. Route T's
von-Mangoldt mass current is the exact pre-divisor carrier: timelike prime frequencies have one
saddle, spacelike frequencies have none, and the glancing null layer remains open. The old
overlap-divisibility target remains retired. Route N contributes receiver and diagnostic invariant,
plus exact coordinates for the source law Route T must control.

### Route O — Connes restricted-Weil/prolate ground space [OPEN]

**Route-level endpoint.** Let `lambda_0(a)` be the bottom of the closed restricted-Weil form on
`L2(-a,a)` and put

~~~
eta(a)=max(0,-lambda_0(a)).
~~~

Nested apertures make `lambda_0` nonincreasing and `eta` nondecreasing. Nonnegativity of every
true floor is equivalent to RH. A cofinal limit `lambda_0(a)->0` also suffices by monotonicity, but
is stronger than needed.

**Lean-closed finite and comparison layers.**

- literal finite restricted-Weil matrix, reflection, real-rooted completion, and selected floor;
- nested-floor monotonicity and no-evacuation: one negative trial vector pins all later floors;

  envelope;

  ~~~

  Q_L(a)^-<=2L eta(a);
  ~~~
- false-RH transverse amplification of the actual negative floor and the exact sampled criterion
  ~~~
  RH
   iff eta(a)=exp(o(a))
   iff sigma_abs(sum_n eta((log n)/2)n^(-s))<=1.
  ~~~
  More quantitatively, every right-transverse Xi displacement forces cofinal exponential growth
  of `eta` and lower-bounds the sampled Dirichlet abscissa.

This advances formal status beyond “concrete radial PSWF realization missing.” Core
special-function primitives, exterior channel, inward regularization, sharp trial-to-floor
domination, transverse amplification, and sampled-growth consumers are Lean-closed. Lean currently

closed CCM operator itself.

**Completed-Evans/log-seam subbranch, formal.** `SuzukiGammaMollifiedEquation.lean` and

||P T beta||^2 = (sum_i w_i^2)(||beta_-||^2+||beta_+||^2),

with prime-power weight sum `sum (log p)^2/p^j`; once `a>(log 2)/2`, every nonzero endpoint trace

Formalized dyadic orbit charts already close compact-middle coverage, and the rank-one

`CCMParityFreeOddGroundFourier.lean` now closes a different, exact finite algebraic gap: for simple

**Provisional prolate-ground and full-charge results.** The full zero-integral positive-even

`RadialPSWFArithmeticDilationDischarge.lean` identifies the diagonal target at the

At `k=n+2`, multiplication by `2 log(k)` is exactly

`ProlateRadialDecay.lean` closes the exact ODE tail that the earlier seed audit still treated as

then transports it to a half-density exponential envelope in logarithmic scale. The `k`th channel

`SuzukiRayleighInfimum.lean` closes a logically separate ground-space ambiguity. The exact

Combining the paper-proved complete radial prime response, Poisson center, critical-Mellin

QW_lambda(a_lambda,a_lambda)
 = D_arch d_lambda log(2*pi*lambda^2)+O(d_lambda),  D_arch>0,

eta(a)=exp(o(a)).

### Route P — completed-seam Thorin/Markov representation [OPEN]

**Endpoint.**

~~~
compact completed-seam Thorin representation
 iff compact completed-seam Markov representation
 iff RH.
~~~

**Proved content.** Markov germ recovers all normalized prime--archimedean moments. Any positive
compact witness is unique and equals canonical reciprocal-square Xi-divisor measure. Theta boundary
transport and Route K all-order survivor both land here. A new route-independent coalitional
attainment theorem gives an exact generic existence test for countable positive carriers: an
actual carrier exists exactly when finite zero-residual coalitions can be chosen uniformly tight,
equivalently bounded in some proper source price. Without a carrier every approximate history
escapes every proper price, and an explicit model shows residual convergence alone is insufficient.
No Route-P source atoms or price satisfying this criterion have been constructed.

**P-G60--P-G97 Lean-proved source and spectral opening.** The actual Gamma-sewn
pre-Gram aperture edge now passes its first hostile source test. Define its inverse tilt `W_s(p)`
from the literal contraction, aperture, and Möbius factors. Direct Lean declarations prove

~~~
W_s(p)(B_s(p,e)-B_0(p,e)) = log(p) p^(-s(e+1)),
sum_(p,e) W_s(p)(B_s(p,e)-B_0(p,e)) = -zeta'(s)/zeta(s)   (s>1).
~~~

For fixed `s>1`, `W_s(p)` tends to zero cofinally; at `s=1`, it exceeds `log p` and tends to
infinity. This kills bounded ambient-prime-Hilbert specialization. The newer Köthe boundary module
then proves the wall is not a source-topology wall: uniformly for `s>=1`, `W_s(p)<=8 log p`; the
signed Kummer-lattice frequency costs at most one prime-log height step; and for every Deninger
height-Köthe vector, the aperture-to-critical difference tends to zero in every defining
projective seminorm. The phase-response module further proves the signed Stokes connection is odd
under Kummer reflection and that every centered Mellin jet, hence the full complex height phase,
has a path-independent right boundary value before norm, Gram, determinant, or reflection folding.
P-G63 proves every fixed-vector response is entire in its phase parameter, so no
fixed nuclear source vector can agree even punctured-locally with the completed logarithmic
derivative at any actual Xi divisor point. Its positive survivor is no longer merely a proposed
codomain. `GammaSewnMobiusApertureStokesDualCurrent.lean` constructs the actual aperture
edge as a coefficient functional continuous against the zeroth height seminorm, with coefficients

coisotropic reduction is one hyperbolic port at every time; `ell1` preserves that port, whereas
every `ell^p`, `p>1`, and `c0` completion erases it after Hausdorff reduction. Finite jet systems
are Vandermonde-complete at matching aperture, but the infinite weak-star orbit has no derivative
in unweighted `ell-infinity`; its generator exists only on logarithmically weighted preduals.
Recurrence, finite tomography, and positive norm therefore do not select a faithful global
completion or an infinitesimal source law. This is a compatibility/no-go theorem, not Route V.

The constitutive universal-law-field modules add exact source mechanics, not an RH route.
`ConstitutiveInteractionSpectrumMechanics.lean` identifies finite interaction work, truncation
tail, and coherence loss, and proves the literal prime formation is summable exactly above
exponent one; reflection across one preserves formation phase only at the fixed point.
`ConstitutiveEssentialFormationMechanics.lean` proves finite or summable compensators cannot move
that wall. `ConstitutivePassiveCycleMechanics.lean` proves every defect vanishes on a closed cycle
whose legs are passive in one work norm. `ConstitutiveStaticHeterogeneityObserver.lean` identifies
posterior variance as the exact dissipation of mean rate and classifies zero variance as active-rate
collapse. These laws could constrain a future source selector, but no declaration types Xi
divisors, completed Euler--Gamma formation, or a physical landing. They therefore neither advance
an existing Tier-1 source theorem nor instantiate Route V.

None of these theorems constrains the landed divisor to
the critical line or supplies an RH endpoint. Route S remains research infrastructure, not yet an
RH proof route.

### Route T — mass-shifted critical outgoing current [OPEN — NEW]

**Endpoint.** For every legal a>1/2, sigma>0, and 1/2<d<a, Lean proves

~~~
RH
 iff uniform boundedness in u>=0 of
    sum_(2<=n<=floor(exp(d*u/(d-1/2))))
      (vonMangoldt(n)-1) n^(-1/2) F_u(log n),
~~~

equivalently uniform boundedness of exact mass-test pairing with finite centered Dirichlet
polynomial.

**Proved mechanism.**

    `(y+c) sum_i rho_i (y+a_i+2c)^(2m-2)/(y-a_i)^(2m) <= G(y)` for `1<=m<=n`; at `m=1`, every
    atom right of `-c` must be financed by Padé mass left of `-c`.  But at `n asymp sqrt(y)` one
    fixed physical residue changes the whole matched disk jet by only `O(rho/sqrt(y))`, or

**Committed polynomial Turan descent and one-point criterion.** The route has advanced past edge
cleansing. `XiResolventWeightedTuranMachine.lean` proves a universal weighted level-cleansing
machine. `XiResolventSpectralDescent.lean` uses real polynomials that vanish on higher real levels
to descend through every finite resolvent-modulus level and proves, at any one shift `X>=0`,

~~~
RH iff for every real polynomial p and every n,
  Re(M_p(n) M_p(n+2) - M_p(n+1)^2) >= 0.
~~~

`XiResolventEvenTuranMachine.lean` and `XiResolventSingleMinorCriterion.lean` remove the `n`
quantifier: polynomial multiplication by powers regenerates the needed even ladder, so the single
index-zero minor for every `p` is equivalent to RH. `XiResolventTraceCauchySchwarz.lean` packages
the same endpoint as

~~~
RH iff for every real polynomials f,g,
  Re(L(f^2)L(g^2)-L(fg)^2) >= 0,
~~~

where `L` is the explicit one-shift arithmetic resolvent trace functional. This is not positivity
on squares: the family is invariant under `L -> -L`, and proof mechanism is spectral oscillation
and polynomial annihilation of already-cleansed levels.

Plain unweighted minors are insufficient. `TuranMinorSubEdgeMasking.lean` gives an explicit
conjugation-symmetric nonreal configuration in the open right unit half-disk with every moment and
every plain consecutive Turan minor nonnegative. Higher real atoms mask the lower nonreal pair;
some source-selective reweighting is necessary. `XiResolventPartialLineSupply.lean` supplies the
`p=1` index-zero inequality only after all atoms above a scale are already real and one explicit
tail-mass versus separated-two-moduli inequality holds. It is a partial-RH bridge, not an
unconditional producer.

`XiResolventEffectiveTuranDetection.lean` and
`XiResolventEffectivePolynomialDescent.lean` make the cleansing finite: explicit gap, mass, and
weight data bound an index beyond which an off-line level forces a negative even minor, and
polynomial killing weights reduce the certificate to one index-zero minor. The terminal family is
smaller still. `XiResolventNodalSingleMinorCriterion.lean` proves, for every fixed `X>=0`,

~~~
RH iff for every finite R subset (0,1] and m>=0,
  the index-zero minor reweighted by (X^m product_(r in R)(X-r))^2 is nonnegative.
~~~

Thus arbitrary real polynomials are a valid receiver but not the minimal open quantifier. Monic
real-rooted nodal squares already detect every off-line level.

`XiResolventRationalNodeCriterion.lean` makes this minimal receiver countable. Uniform source-
moment continuity under node perturbation proves that it suffices to take a node count `j`, an
exponent `m`, and a rational tuple `c : Fin j -> Q` with every coordinate in `(0,1]`. Rational
density recovers the real nodal inequalities, and Lean records countability of the complete index
type. The terminal criterion is therefore a countable arithmetic inequality family, not an
uncountable positivity wall.

`XiResolventNodalSupply.lean` now prices each member of that countable family. If all divisor atoms
above a chosen resolvent scale are already on the line and an explicit two-scale tail-mass bound
holds, then the weighted index-zero Turan minor is nonnegative. For a rational nodal datum the
budget is explicit: its adverse tail cost grows as `2^(4j)` and its witness reserve contains the
degree factor `(r_1 r_2)^(2m+1)`, the squared node-distance products, and
`(r_1-r_2)^2`. This is a conditional source-supply theorem, not an RH proof: the corpus does not
yet establish its line-placement and mass hypotheses uniformly for every datum.

`HTGeometry/TuranMinorNodeRefinementNonmonotone.lean` closes a tempting shortcut. An explicit
conjugation-symmetric three-atom configuration has a nonnegative plain Turan minor but a strictly
negative one-node refinement. Nodal refinement therefore has no monotone order from which the
countable signs can be inducted; the family must be exhausted datum by datum or controlled by a
different global invariant.

**Literal countable arithmetic landing.** Lean-proved
`XiShiftedNodalAdaptiveArithmeticSourceLanding.lean` closes the source-formation interface for
that family. For each weight `p`, the division-free successor

~~~
Q = L(p^2) (X p) - L(p X p) p
~~~

is orthogonal to `p`, and its mixed landing is exactly the weighted Turan determinant. At square
shift it is also exactly one common-kernel rational--Gamma-minus-von-Mangoldt packet formed from
`(p,Q)` before reflection aggregation. The moving-admission derivative is minus the literal
bilinear completed arithmetic source plus the exact admission-connection source. For every fixed
`w>1/2`, Lean proves RH equivalent both to nonnegativity of all real nodal landings and to
nonnegativity of the countable rational-node subfamily. These equivalences do not prove a sign.

**Formal horizontal-jet and contact-cascade laws.** Lean-proved
`XiShiftedHorizontalNodalJetCurvature.lean` removes moving-admission bookkeeping from the same endpoint. Every Taylor jet at horizontal
parameter zero is an exact ascending-factorial multiple of the next polynomial-weighted shifted-Xi
moment. Its denominator-free second-jet curvature is a strictly positive degree factor times the
weighted Turan landing; at base mass `exp(1)` it is exactly the literal completed rational--Gamma-
minus-von-Mangoldt landing. This is a connection-free geometric realization of the existing
obstruction, not a curvature-sign theorem.

`XiShiftedProjectiveNodalContactCascade.lean` supplies the complementary moving law. Projective

covariant_rate(D)=4a D-(2n+3)(M_0 M_3-M_1 M_2).

At a flat contact `D=0`, the remaining higher compound is exactly the next source-first completed

`HTGeometry/TuranMinorDetectionDelay.lean` proves two exact finite facts: a
single off-line conjugate pair has a negative
minor at every index, while one explicit real-atom-plus-pair configuration has nonnegative minors

**Hard-edge and flat-contact source laws.** The committed
`XiShiftedHardEdgeAdaptiveSourceTransversality.lean` identifies literal shifted-Xi Gauss/Padé

`XiShiftedCanonicalAdmissionHardEdgeWorldlineSourceLaw.lean` strengthens this to an exact

tends to zero and the compact-normalized hard derivative tends to `d`, then effective source

`XiShiftedCanonicalAdmissionFlatContactSourceTransmutation.lean` treats the tangential face. The

These laws preserve arithmetic forcing at a flat face but do not produce positive-chamber or

**Revised open core.** The parabolic first-contact scalar estimate, unsquared carrier, finite Hankel

`A_n'=-2n A_(n+1)`. Dividing apex-square by its canonical degree/mass factor gives a nonnegative

Projective synchronization is sharper. Fixed low Jacobi edges force root-spread divergence on

The later cofinal finite-Hankel chain supersedes this formulation. One top source account pays the

False RH is equivalent to one explicit cofinal family of negative finite Hankel packets with
dimension `N(n,k)=(n+1)(k+1)+1`; arbitrary packet-size escape is closed.
`XiShiftedHardEdgeReciprocalClearingHyperbolicity.lean` classifies reciprocal-cleared zeros:

The newest source bridge changes the remaining object. For every shifted-reversal polynomial the

a^2 Q(x^2)^2/(a^2+x^2)^(2d+1)

Negative capital therefore contains an actual pointwise completed-source inversion, and the

~~~
not RH
  iff one fixed polynomial--Gaussian carrier has completed prime--Gamma inversions
      at every sufficiently large hard-edge mass.

The first source theorem is now: **exclude this fixed-carrier cofinal super-aperture inversion
phase using the degree-free Cauchy density and the intact Gamma--polar--von-Mangoldt incidence.**
Generic Hankel positivity, root hyperbolicity, scalar absolute-tail bounds, determinant-line
budgets, local transversality, unweighted spread, raw reciprocal clocks, positive normalization,

The frozen `U-DYADIC-SOURCE` conjecture resolves what a first cross-scale theorem would have to

~~~
L(((1/2)E+O)^2)=(1/4)L(E^2)+L(O^2),
~~~

so the flat gap is precisely the nonnegative odd-detail energy. But Lean also proves the hostile
obstruction. If `x_a(z)=(a^2-z^2)/(a^2+z^2)`, then applying `T_2` after doubling mass gives a

~~~
T_2(x_(2a)(z))-x_a(z)
 = 2 z^4(z^2-8a^2)/((4a^2+z^2)^2(a^2+z^2)).
~~~

The quadratic drift cancels, but Route U still evaluates coarse and fine packets at different
nodes and with different completed resolvent weights. Thus flat arcsine Pythagoras cannot be

`XiShiftedHardEdgeDyadicSampleCoherence.lean` now closes the pointwise analytic part of that

For every fixed compact-support Schwartz approximation and every Xi zero copy, the full fine

The literal first target is a weighted square/cross-divisor exchange theorem with a genuine
summable majorant (`U-DYC-global`). That promoted energy/covariance statement—not pointwise sample convergence—is now the first
cross-scale producer capable of excluding the negative fixed point.

The quadratic-shell hostile audit now changes that target.  Lean proves that the fine shell has
ultraviolet parity `(E,O)->(h,-h)`, while the full fine reconstruction tends to zero.  Rotating to

The strongest positive survivor is the unique signed class modulo `S^2`,

~~~
J=S D=(E+O)(E-O)=E^2-O^2.
~~~

`XiShiftedHardEdgeNyquistNullForm.lean` proves its exact algebra and that `J` tends to zero both for
every fixed Xi divisor copy (infrared parity `(1,1)`) and every positive quadratic shell
(ultraviolet parity `(1,-1)`).  `XiShiftedHardEdgeGlobalNyquistCurrentExchange.lean` now closes the
missing complete-divisor transition uniformly.  At fine phase `z`, coarse alias period
`L=pi*(k+1)`, fine packet `P_f`, and coarse packet `P_c`, Lean proves exactly

~~~
J=P_f(P_c-P_f),
norm(J)<=K/L^4
~~~

for one source-dependent `K` independent of scale and Xi copy.  In the inner coarse cell the common

makes `P_f` small while exact alias periodicity folds `P_c` into a bounded central cell.  Retaining
the native fine-scale shifted-resolvent weight gives a reciprocal-square divisor majorant with
coefficient

~~~
4K/(pi^4*(k+1)^2) -> 0.
~~~

Therefore the full weighted `XiZeroCopy` sum of `J` is absolutely summable at every scale and tends
to zero unconditionally.  No Riemann--von Mangoldt theorem or zero-location premise is used.  The
higher principle is a two-chart null product: one factor is uniformly small everywhere, but the
small factor switches at the Nyquist wall.  The earlier zero-count obstruction was a shadow of
taking norms before forming this complementary product.

This closes the analytic `U-DYC-global` exchange but also shows that native weighted `J` alone is a
vanishing reporter, not a nonzero positive producer.  The successor
`XiShiftedHardEdgePrimeGammaNyquistFlux.lean` now closes the finite-scale source identity before
divisor aggregation.  The naive difference of independently minimal even/odd source capitals is
wrong: their reciprocal lifts can have different degrees, hence different factorials and positive
Laplace filters.  Lean pads both lifts to the common degree

~~~
D=max(deg R_E,deg R_O),
~~~

constructs the actual fine even Chebyshev packet directly from physical even lattice modes, and
defines the odd packet as exact residual.  At every Xi copy their polynomial values are exactly
`E,O`.  With both literal Gamma--polar--von-Mangoldt capitals evaluated under the same filter,
Lean proves

~~~
a_f^2 (C_E-C_O)
 = pi (2D)! Re sum_q W_f(q)(E(q)^2-O(q)^2).
~~~

Thus the mass/factorial-renormalized literal prime--Gamma parity flux equals the complete Nyquist
current and tends to zero.  Orientation is preserved all the way to source landing; no unidentified
boundary remainder survives.  The higher source principle is **degree as gauge**: common-degree
padding is the covariant comparison, while minimal-degree subtraction manufactures a fake
factorial anomaly.

This sharpens rather than solves the route. Vanishing reconstruction/detail Nyquist flux is
compatible with negative source capital. Post-cutoff
`XiShiftedHardEdgePrimeGammaAntipodalPolarization.lean` now supplies the correct polarization.
It constructs the symmetric reciprocal-Hankel bilinear form, the literal completed prime--Gamma
cross balance, and the physical antipodal even/odd decomposition. Hard-edge inversion is exactly
antipodal in the Cayley coordinate, Chebyshev modes diagonalize it, and the degree-free Cauchy
density has the exact inversion Jacobian. For the actual cofinal packet, negative full capital
forces one labelled alternative: negative even diagonal capital, negative odd diagonal capital, or
negative even--odd cross capital; each yields a pointwise negative completed prime--Gamma balance
of the same label. Lean proves

```text
not RH <-> cofinal fixed-carrier antipodal prime--Gamma polarization phase.
```

Exact Route-U open core is therefore source-specific exclusion of all three labelled alternatives
for that fixed carrier. The old “diagonal only” phrasing was incomplete; polarization shows the
cross channel must remain intact. Universal exclusion is RH itself and cannot be a premise.

`XiShiftedHardEdgePrimeGammaKelvinCrossCurrent.lean` now resolves geometry of that cross channel.
Common-degree shifted reversal converts hard-edge antipody into Kelvin reciprocity.  If `Q_E,Q_O`
are actual even/odd reversed source polynomials, `A=a^2`, and `d` is their common reciprocal degree,
Lean proves

```text
Q_E(A^2/W) =  (A/W)^d Q_E(W),
Q_O(A^2/W) = -(A/W)^d Q_O(W).
```

Consequently literal Gaussian cross density `F_t(x)=Q_E(x^2)Q_O(x^2)e^(-t x^2)` reverses sign on
every physical inversion orbit `x <-> a^2/x` and vanishes at self-dual seam `x=a`.  Lean also
identifies `F_t` pointwise with exact norm polarization inside completed prime--Gamma balance and
factors Jacobian-paired current for any weight `w` as

```text
F_t(x) * [w(x) - (a^2/x^2)^(2d+1)
  exp(-t*((a^2/x)^2-x^2)) w(a^2/x)].
```

Thus pointwise sign control and naive inversion cancellation are both false.  Cross branch is an
oriented modular current, not hidden energy.  Sharpened open core is compare this exact Gamma--heat
modular defect (`w=gammaHardyPhaseRate`) with polar and prime cross terms of same completed-source
identity.  Dropping prime cross polarization would replay the diagonal error.

`XiShiftedHardEdgePrimeGammaKelvinMellinCriticalPhase.lean` now removes Kelvin cocycle exactly.  The
forced zero-heat normalization

```text
K(x)=(a/x)^(2d) F_0(x)
```

is anti-invariant, `K(a^2/x)=-K(x)`, so `H(u)=K(a exp(u))` is literally odd.  For inner log-radius
`x=a exp(-u)`, `u>0`, Lean reduces unit-weight paired heat defect to

```text
D_(a,d,t)(u)=1-exp(2*(2d+1)*u-2*t*a^2*sinh(2u)).
```

It has unique positive critical time

```text
t_*(a,d,u)=((2d+1)*u)/(a^2*sinh(2u)),
```

with `D<0` below `t_*`, `D=0` at `t_*`, and `D>0` above `t_*`; positive heat times of both
orientations exist on every positive orbit.  Hence no heat-uniform cross orientation is available.
This classifies modular defect, not full current sign: full current remains `F_t D`, and theorem uses
`w=1`, not Gamma rate.  Live cross target is therefore an integrated signed heat-mass comparison
relative to orbit-dependent `t_*`, retaining literal Gamma rate, polar residue, and prime Fourier
cross source.

`XiShiftedHardEdgePrimeGammaKelvinLaplaceDetailedBalance.lean` closes that integrated heat-mass
comparison exactly.  If `d` is actual common reciprocal degree, `y=a^2/x`, and `C_t(x;w)` is
Jacobian-paired cross current, Lean proves

```text
integral_(0,infinity) t^(2d) exp(-a^2 t) C_t(x;w) dt
 = (2d)! F_0(x)/(a^2+x^2)^(2d+1) * (w(x)-w(y)).
```

Thus source filter does not retain a signed distribution relative to pointwise critical time: its
rate `a^2`, moment order `2d`, Kelvin exponent `2d+1`, and Jacobian form exact detailed balance and
erase heat cocycle orbit by orbit.  Unit weight cancels identically.  For actual Gamma rate and

rate away from self-dual seam.  Generic carrier orientation is false (`1+X` and `1-X` reverse odd
channel), and parent Xi-filtered transmutation already contains global degree cancellation; this is
a physical-orbit `[BRIDGE]`, not claimed new mechanism.  Sharpened cross core is now solely literal
prime--polar completion versus this pure Gamma commutator for fixed cofinal carrier.  Pointwise heat
phase analysis must not be used as independent source resource.

`XiShiftedHardEdgePrimeGammaKelvinTwoChartNoGo.lean` closes global scalar completion negatively.
For the fixed log-radius interior chart and fixed physical boundary chart, respectively, Lean proves

```text
gammaHardyPhaseRate(a*exp(-1))-gammaHardyPhaseRate(a*exp(1)) -> -1,
gammaHardyPhaseRate(1)-gammaHardyPhaseRate(a^2)+log(a)
  -> gammaHardyPhaseRate(1)+log(2*pi)/2.
```

It then proves, premise-free, that no function `r : R -> R` can be added to both expressions so
that both converge to finite real limits.  This excludes every global scalar reserve, without
regularity assumptions—not merely multiples of `log(a)`.  The higher organizing principle is a
two-chart logarithmic charge: zero on interior Mellin orbits and one on physical boundary.  Any
surviving completed source must therefore be orbit/chart-dependent before scalar landing.  Exact
open core is to derive and cancel that non-scalar charge from literal polar evaluation plus the
von-Mangoldt Fourier cross source for one fixed false-RH carrier; the Gamma asymptotic alone does
not sign those arithmetic terms.

**Earlier equivalent structural formulation.** In the degenerate/root-sign branch, items
55--58 replace unspecified odd descent by a literal Padé spectral-measure and source-capacity
statement. Define
`mu_j^+=sum_(u_(j,i)<0) (omega_(j,i)/x_(j,i)) delta_(-u_(j,i))`. Prove
`mu_j^+(K)->0` on every compact `K` in `(0,infinity)`, or the equivalent source-side vanishing of
the Poisson boundary atoms. This is weaker than full first-Hausdorff positivity: it permits
super-apex nodes whose residues collapse or whose hard coordinates escape. It excludes every
simple negative hard root with `gamma_r>0` by the exact residue limit. If `gamma_r=0`, use the
resulting parity-doubled terminal radical instead. Full odd-sector operator admission would still
require liminf control on the complete odd form domain.  Equivalently at a simple limiting root,
exclude a finite nonzero zero-edge response of the literal source `cosh(kappa t)`; forcing a
ground pole collapses the Padé residue but must then be combined with the explicit degeneracy
contradiction.  Equivalently, rule out the `sqrt(tau)` Lorentzian regularized-capacity spike
uniformly on every compact positive `kappa` interval.  Generic reduced-resolvent positivity,
pointwise control off the roots, and unscaled capacity averaging do not decide which alternative
occurs.

Odd or degenerate Connes--van Suijlekom extension is also closed as a stand-alone mechanism.
Positive terminal mass makes `i kappa` common only to the even kernel; it does not constrain an odd
lower-ground vector there.  Moreover item 59 proves that a simple odd lower ground is itself
Fourier-real-rooted, so odd descent supplies no contradiction.  A viable use of the full-ground
theorem must first prove from the literal completed-Xi source that the selected even boundary state
is the full bottom, or derive a new cross-parity identity forcing its nonreal zero onto every lower
odd ground.

Item 60b further removes a hidden premise: whenever the positive-mass branch really descends below
the even floor, the odd lower ground is automatically unique and simple.  Its Fourier transform is
real-rooted and its reciprocal gap is one exact cross-source current, but neither invariant forces
vanishing at the even imaginary root.  Any successor invoking odd-ground degeneracy or merely
assuming odd simplicity is therefore obsolete.

The positive-real Padé lane is now equally sharp.  Item 60 proves that ordinary convergence in
capacity is too weak by one conformal derivative: it sees `rho_tilde->0` automatically while the
physical atom is the normalized quantity `rho_tilde/(1+z)`.  Carathéodory positivity supplies a
new exact reserve law, but a bounded positive atom needs only a vanishing fraction of the large
left-pole reservoir at the critical degree scale.  A closing theorem must therefore control the
Julia/Carleson boundary atom and its left-reserve payment using the literal prime--Gamma source;
no finite matched-jet or unweighted disk-capacity theorem can suffice.  Item 60a also closes the
obvious finite-head repair: enlarging the analytic half-plane inserts asymptotically identical
opposite boundary jets, so depletion has no stand-alone Carathéodory leverage.

Contact orientation is closed as a mechanism: every first Padé defect gives the same signed first
nonzero derivative to every hard node, but a positive derivative at a flat face does not make its
level nonnegative. In the simple-birth branch, the parent radical-jet theorem already merges the
problem with the literal simple-even ground sign; more root division, contact parity, or generic
Laguerre--Polya theory is theatre unless it produces a new prime--Gamma/global-Xi anchor. Fixed-
degree arcsine recession, weak convergence, and bulk quadrature cannot close either branch because
a vanishing ordinary weight may retain nonzero absolute Padé residue after hard blow-up.

The current-Christoffel outgoing-ray route remains an alternate producer,
but items 51--52 close its fixed-mass comparison and cancel all explicit admission velocity from
the mass-varying source debt. Item 53 proves generic positive moment flow cannot control the
remaining odd defect-energy drift plus current norm flux. Only a label-sensitive prime--Gamma or
global completed-Xi law can revive this lane. It is not logically required for the selected least-defect sequence. Fixed-face plateau,
amplitude gauge, coordinate gauge, sub/superparabolic least-defect escape, and translation to
spectral infinity are closed.

A Stage-1 successor proposes an alternate U-native formulation: the parabolic degree lower bound
puts terminal positivity within one bounded relative mass interval, so after static root division
is exhausted the remaining datum is adjacent frozen-to-current admission transport on that bounded
interval. Item 51 closes arithmetic formation and every fixed-mass comparison. Missing theorem is
now a literal arithmetic prime--Gamma/global-Xi quantitative or sign law for the item-52 odd
defect-energy/norm-flux expression along the selected path; generic positive resolvent-flow
arguments are false by item 53. It
is therefore a live refinement of the same parity-admission wall, not a new route or a proved
replacement open core; its abstract root-deletion wrapper receives no formal credit.

**Dead subclasses.** Generic moment positivity, positive atoms, tied-power sectors, positive/root
location, stochastic order, factorial-normalized carriers, fixed corona continuous reporters,
Gamma lifetime, per-orbit three-point positivity, fixed angular sectors, endpoint-height control,
reflection aggregation, unweighted total pair energy, and a post-contact fourth-cumulant reserve
all admit exact counterexamples, merely transport existing sign, or are equivalent to the terminal
sign. Absolute monic margins, amplitude-only normalization, coordinate-dilated recession, and
generic Gauss positivity likewise do not control moving admission shape.

**Why independent.** U is a first-crossing extension problem on an adaptive finite null flag.
Route G instead needs a cofinal two-angle meromorphic source and Xi transfer; P needs one global
compact positive measure. Neither route's producer implies U's adaptive Euler--Gamma crossing
orientation.

## 5. Candidate routes not yet instantiated

### Post-J principal-relative current

Would be new route only after literal source object, non-coboundary incidence, and unconditional RH
handoff are constructed. Current concrete realization is R.

### XiC order-one explicit-formula organ [SHARED INFRASTRUCTURE — NOT A ROUTE]

The former growth frontier is closed in committed Lean. `CompletedZetaGrowth.lean` proves
the unconditional global order-one envelope `xi_growth_order_le_one`; centered coordinate change
gives the corresponding XiC bound. `XiHadamardGrowth.lean` proves quotient constancy and the exact
multiplicity-faithful product

~~~
XiC = XiC(0) * xiCanonicalProduct,
~~~

with no residual exponential factor. `XiZerosInfinite.lean` proves the completed divisor support
infinite, while `XiHeatShellMinimum.lean` and the canonical-product layer supply shell finiteness
and inverse-square summability. Thus growth, Hadamard factorization, and nontrivial-zero existence
are not open.

The prime side is also Lean-proved: `XiWardInteriorTraceSplit.lean` proves on `Re s>1`
that `logDeriv xi` is its two pole terms plus `logDeriv GammaR` minus the von Mangoldt L-series;
`XiGuinandWeilSymmetricContour.lean` identifies the centered bottom edge with the exact
prime--archimedean source, and finite-contour modules give the zero-residue receiver. Moreover,
Lean-proved `XiRelativeResolventTrace.lean` already gives the absolutely convergent
centered zero-side partial fraction in relative-resolvent form.

`XiExplicitFormulaLogDeriv.lean` transports that formula to classical
coordinates and proves, wherever `xi(s) != 0`,

~~~
logDeriv xi(s)
  = sum_rho (1/(rho-1/2) + 1/(s-rho)),
~~~

and glues it on `Re s>1` to the pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt side as an unconditional zero-sum =
prime-sum identity. This direct classical-coordinate explicit formula is now formally certified;
it is shared infrastructure, not a missing proof route. Only a live route's independent
source-side positivity, coercivity, or cancellation theorem could turn these identities into RH
leverage.

`XiWeilResolventChartDictionary.lean` now gives an exact base-chart weld. At
imaginary Cauchy parameter `-it`, each Weil pair
summand is the resolvent point at shift `t^2`; hence the diagonal Weil value is the zeroth
resolvent moment. Lean derives its strict positive real part, identifies it for `t>0`
with `logDeriv xi(1/2+t)/t`, and for `t>1/2` with the explicit pole--Gamma--von-Mangoldt
expression. These declarations receive formal credit. They orient only this Cauchy-test diagonal,
not the full Weil form or higher resolvent Hankel tower, so they weld C1/Weil and resolvent
infrastructure without closing a route or creating Route V.

`XiResolventTowerLowDegreePositivity.lean` extends this weld formally. It places every shifted squared spectral point
in the open 45-degree cone for nonnegative shift and in the required 30-degree cone for shifts at
least `3/4`. Hence the first resolvent moment has strictly positive real part for every
nonnegative shift and the second does so for shifts at least `3/4`; an exact power-kernel
dictionary transfers these statements to squared and cubed Cauchy-test Weil diagonals. This is not
full Weil positivity: degree two at small shift and the higher tower remain unoriented. These
declarations receive formal credit but do not change any route endpoint.

### Order-one pre-Gamma Sonin successor

Would replace F's wrong-order finite-height transfer. No source operator and chain exist yet.

### Conservative Euler-shell/Witt scale completion

This is the closest new Route-V candidate at the cutoff, but it is not yet instantiated. Lean
constructs literal Möbius prime-exponent formation edges, their conservation/reconstruction laws,
polar criticality, and a bilateral-summability selector whose surviving radial character is
unitary. It also constructs pole-unit memory, the Gamma base, and the Euler-shell specialization.
The conservative inverse-completion theorem is exact: if finite formation and exterior companion
stages are inverse pairs and converge jointly in one Hausdorff multiplicative completion, the
inverse identity survives, so every multiplicative receiver sees a zero-free completed formation.
The literal pointwise Euler-shell specialization is now Lean-proved and classified exactly:
for every `m>=12`, pointwise conservative completion on the open right half-strip is equivalent to
RH. This is a terminal gate, not a source theorem; it deliberately forgets that the stages must be
source-formed and converge jointly in one completion.

`MarkedEulerShellSkeleton.lean` and `MarkedEulerShellWall.lean` strengthen the pre-zero source
side while proving a new obstruction. The marked exponent shell is coefficient-faithful on its
pure-prime skeleton, has a positive Mellin--GNS realization, compact resolvent, recurrent observed
orbit, divergent unwrapped history, and an HT realization. But no finite positive normalization
reaches the physical Mass half-density prime vector: shell summability requires `sigma>1`, while
matching `(log p)/sqrt(p)` forces `sigma=1/m<=1/12`; pure-prime reporting without the marked mixed
shell is not coefficient-faithful. Thus positivity, recurrence, and compact resolvent do not fill
the candidate's source-to-physical normalization crossing.

The missing source object is decisive: no committed theorem constructs the finite source-generated
inverse pair and proves its joint convergence on the half-plane needed by the endpoint. A further
no-go forbids hiding this in one ordinary Banach algebra with a bounded derivation satisfying
`D T=1`; the viable completion must be scale-, sheaf-, or locally convex. Until those literal
stages and their joint admission are built, this is a proved receiver architecture rather than an
independent route.

### Triptic orbital-divisor normal-form successor

Lean now gives a complete finite source quotient. Every mixed-constraint periodic meromorphic
source differs from an explicit finite elliptic-gamma jet correction by a differentially
algebraic meromorphic remainder, and every finite orbital divisor datum is realized. The additive
divisor is a finite class function with one jet basis element per orbit class and order. This is a
source-classification theorem, not merely special-function notation. The torus invariant-field
layer is also complete: arbitrary protocols retain velocity plus annihilating Laurent monomials,
while an expanding protocol leaves only a differentially independent velocity whose intersection
with palette rational functions is constant. The concrete presentation gap is closed as well:
elliptic-gamma cone and multiplicative presentations agree after the explicit coordinate shift,
meromorphic realization commutes with translation, logarithmic derivative, and every jet, and any
nonzero finite jet combination, including the untranslated base-source class, is formally
hypertranscendental. The remaining kernel ambiguity is now closed: a source is differentially
algebraic exactly when every additive orbital-divisor coefficient vanishes; for multiplicative
sources, its logarithmic derivative is differentially algebraic exactly when every triptic orbit
charge vanishes.

The Kummer layer completes this source quotient rather than creating an RH handoff.
`TripticMultiplicativeSourceClass`, `TripticSourceDivisorFinsupp`,
`TripticGammaProductRealization`, `TripticKummerRootClassification`,
`TripticSourceReciprocity`, and `TripticSourceClassGroup` prove that every finite integral orbit
divisor is realized; an `m`th source root modulo differentially algebraic factors exists exactly
when `m` divides every divisor coefficient; Kummer content is the exact maximal root order; and
the quotient by the differentially algebraic kernel is the full finite divisor group. The
separate irrational-triptic effectivity character reconstructs its HT germ but is explicitly not
a zeta/Xi result.

No committed theorem maps this quotient coefficient-faithfully to the Xi divisor or supplies a
reflection/polarization law forcing critical orientation. Those two constructions would instantiate
a genuinely new route because its first producer would be orbital-divisor exhaustion, not a
classical positivity or scalar criterion. It remains a Route-V candidate, not an instantiated
route: richer source classification does not supply the Xi map or orient its reflection class.

### Tropical-projective source-normal descent

Lean now gives a third Route-V candidate core. For any finite nonempty family of exact
source actions, mandatory maximum work has a nonnegative projective width; width vanishes exactly
when every parent increment agrees, is invariant under a common potential, and controls descent of
the full relative action profile through an observer. Closed histories have nonnegative work. For
finite prime clocks, arbitrarily late phase returns coexist with projective width growing at least
linearly with elapsed time and the logarithmic clock span. For a centered affine parameter and a
nonzero clock span, zero width is exactly the centered state.

This is not Route V. The centered-width theorem does not mention Xi, and the recurrence theorem
does not force observer descent. A route would require one source-normal Xi observer constructed
before divisor specialization, proof that the complete prime projective profile factors through
it at every Xi zero, and a coefficient-faithful identification of its zero-width fibre with the
physical centered state. No such observer, descent theorem, or RH handoff exists.

### Nyman--Beurling / Baez--Duarte finite sections

New Lean corpus formalizes criterion, Gram matrices, detectors, least squares, finite-section
determinants, Möbius residuals, and obstructions. This is important infrastructure but classical

as Route V.

### Adaptive eigenframe source memory

Lean now proves exact two-query Pauli twirling, Haar mean/tail laws, and permanent
fixed-body Majorana separation: for every fixed positive even body order, every nonzero autonomous
Hamiltonian with that support stays strictly above the Haar score at every time once qubit count
is large enough. The generator-matched form is stronger and dimension-only: every nonzero
traceless autonomous `n`-qubit Hamiltonian retains its full adjoint autocorrelation at every time,
whereas Haar probability of retaining even half that amplitude is at most
`4/((2^n)^2-1)`; the dictionary signal and Haar separation hold simultaneously. This is a genuine
autonomous source-memory mechanism. It becomes an RH route only after constructing an arithmetic
Xi-typed Hamiltonian or source, preserving divisor and multiplicity provenance, and proving a
coefficient-faithful handoff to an RH endpoint. None exists at this cutoff, so it is a candidate
grammar rather than Route V.

### Coefficient-faithful Helson Fredholm-wall response

Lean contains a substantial candidate architecture. For every
divisor-summable coefficient stream `a`, `HelsonFredholmWall.lean` constructs the literal
trace-class Helson operator `K_a(j,k)=a_(jk)` on `l2`, proves coefficient faithfulness and
self-adjointness for real streams, identifies the quadratic response with the divisor convolution
`W_u(n)=sum_(jk=n) conj(u_j)u_k`, and uses the compact Fredholm alternative to characterize every
nonzero spectral wall. At a simple wall, the eigenvalue derivative in coefficient direction `h`
is `sum_n h_n W_u(n)`; the wall gap therefore has a continuous nonzero crossing covector. The
least active eigenvector coordinate supplies an explicit detecting coefficient at its square.
Normalized Mellin exchange tangents feed this crossing form directly. Generic compact-operator
wall language has thus become a coefficient-faithful arithmetic mechanism.

This is not yet Route V. Thresholds can be fitted to arbitrary eigenvalues, distinct eigenlines
give distinct response covectors, coefficient positivity does not imply operator positivity, and
the constant stream fails the required divisor summability. No theorem constructs a canonical
completed-Xi coefficient stream in this chart, identifies a fixed nonzero spectral threshold whose
avoidance is equivalent to RH, or orients/avoids every wall globally, including multiple
eigenvalues. The full entry/self-adjoint/response/wall stack is Lean-proved. Promotion to a
route requires all four missing data:

1. literal source-derived Xi/prime--Gamma coefficient stream with proved divisor summability;
2. fixed RH-equivalent Fredholm threshold, not one selected after seeing the spectrum;
3. source law giving the crossing sign and excluding tangency/multiple-wall escape;
4. global continuation proving no forbidden wall is crossed from a known reference chamber.

Until then this is the strongest current candidate architecture, not a
twelfth live route.

### Longitudinal--transverse source selection gate

Lean now decomposes each complete-resolution source into a marked longitudinal divisor
coordinate and a zero-codiagonal transverse radical. For every continuous linear intervention,
the output codiagonal is exactly its marked-section response plus a uniquely defined mixing defect.
Vanishing mixing is equivalent to preserving the radical; with scalar section action, such an
intervention can only rescale the already-present divisor. The literal complete-shell
specialization proves that arbitrary transverse perturbations leave the pole-unit scale response
unchanged.

This is a cross-route mechanism law, not Route V. It rules out normalized shell manipulation,
radical-preserving completion, or transverse source enrichment as a selector by itself. A new route
could begin only with a source-generated intervention having nonzero longitudinal--transverse
mixing, followed by reflected analytic admission and a coefficient-faithful Xi handoff. No such
intervention is instantiated.

### Cofinal port, inertia, and phase-memory receivers

New Lean theorems identify limiting inverse capacity with admission, its zero set with the
closed vertical fibre, and closability with nondegeneracy; divergent weighted capacity gives dense

Witt-rank obstruction. Finite lattice directions force rational verticality, but a cofinally

extrapolation. Finite unitary/Choi/Pauli and phase-action laws retain source memory, but an

### Source-coherent multi-organ synchronization

Lean now proves a finite selection grammar that survives independent-organ gauge no-gos. Pairwise

anchor completes reconstruction. For a family of source response maps, synchronization selects

through partitions and received by Boolean, mass, Hankel, and quantum-information charts.

This is not Route V. No committed theorem constructs Xi-typed organ maps whose divisor

the generic finite theorem does not.

### Euler q-Gamma divisor-translation process

This is a real new source architecture but not yet a proof route. It begins with positive
prime-local q-Gamma laws, produces an ordinary random-integer law, continues its global response

unit translation. The cyclic GNS observer is recurrent and has compact diagonal resolvent. The
response and its continuations are now Lean-proved hypertranscendental. The genus-one exchange is
also fully certified: renormalized local factors are quadratically close to one, their prime
product is multipliable, locally uniformly convergent, holomorphic and zero-free on
`Re(s)>1/(2 sigma)-1`, and the natural renormalization erases the translation divisor.

The abelian zeta-gas layer supplies its pre-zero source. Prime-power geometric modes give the
zeta-Gibbs law on finite abelian groups; order/temperature dilation and socle/fugacity translation
are distinct response axes, and the q-Gamma random-integer law is the exact socle shadow. Lean

condensation walls, local and global socle law, mode and gas expectations, and the additive
von-Mangoldt/`-zeta'/zeta` residue chain. The full response has a finite-depth divisor formula and
a convergent, nonzero, holomorphic depth tail, hence an exact meromorphic order formula away from
the natural accumulation boundary. It also proves that accumulation line is a genuine natural
boundary, constructs the gas word/KMS algebra with a unique admissible Gibbs state, and realizes

continuity, the canonical tilt density and Radon--Nikodym/log-density formulas, measurability of
the class map, equality of class law with the gas pushforward, and socle expectation transport.
The entropy layer is now closed too: log-socle and both log-likelihood ratios are integrable, both
directed Kullback--Leibler divergences are evaluated, and their symmetrized value is exactly
`sigma` times the von-Mangoldt Dirichlet mass, equivalently the corresponding logarithmic zeta
derivative expression. The Lean-proved squarefull-sector module identifies the depth-two
wall at `1/2` and constructs the corresponding almost surely finite random abelian-group law;
this is a source phase boundary, not a zero-location theorem, because both order and activity
observers remain divisor-blind. The Gibbs Gram kernel, positivity on `a* a`, product-reversing star,
completed GNS representation, generated C-star algebra, positive cyclic vector state, and its
continuous normalized KMS uniqueness on the whole generated C-star algebra are now all
Lean-proved. The exact modulus analysis is now also formal in Lean: the complete
nonnegative depth-by-harmonic defect ladder is summable, equals the q-Pochhammer/q-Gamma
log-modulus defect, has leading term `q(1-cos theta)`, and differs from it by a remainder summable
over primes throughout `sigma>1/2`. Vacuum-weight bounds give probability at least `q/2` to one
copy empty and the other occupied, and prove these symmetrization probabilities are not summable
for `1/2<sigma<=1`. This is the exact Borel--Cantelli divergence input, not yet its product-measure
wrapper or a zero-location theorem. The modulus-budget module is now Lean-proved: phase
gauge leaves modulus unchanged, while the genus-one counterterm pays the leading defect up to an
absolutely summable correction for `sigma>1/2`. The formalized prime-window theorem proves PNT in fixed
multiplicative/phase windows, divergence of the harmonic window sum, and nonsummability of the
leading defect for every nonzero phase when `0<sigma<=1`. The formalized continuation transfers
this divergence to the full q-Gamma defect, proves its partial sums tend to infinity, the

remain normalized at phase zero. These results supply a genuine
positive operator-algebraic source and a sharp probabilistic/analytic continuation boundary, not
a critical-line orientation.

A new law-control layer extracts a marked source commutator from this gas. Lean now proves that
exchanging temperature translation with source-selected socle tilt generates the pure fugacity
parameter translation. Lean also proves the socle response quotient, its exact translation law,
safe-chamber holomorphy/zero-freeness, hypertranscendence, and complete finite-order divisor law.
Positive fugacity entropy, recurrence/open lift, and any critical-line handoff remain open. The
mechanism is explicitly not RH.

A second comparison prevents overgeneralizing that commutator mechanism. The gas realizes
law genesis by noncommuting endpoint schedules, whereas the elliptic-gamma source has commuting
endpoint translations and trivial pure two-shift Koszul defect but a nonzero mixed
coefficient/Čech transgression. The proposed common grammar must therefore retain execution

What is missing is not another landing or special-function theorem. No source law relates these

### Gauss quasi-inner multi-place assembly

This candidate has advanced from a formal finite-place sketch to a complete local asymptotic

### Cassini--Wold / Deninger lower-half weld

This Lean-proved architecture is a real opening, but route-equivalence is already proved.
The Stieltjes--Cassini defect is a finite nonnegative sum that vanishes
exactly under RH; its phase-clock frequency is the mixed horizontal-grade/physical-height source
jet, and its BV--Wold landing is an exact positive Fisher energy. At critical half density, a
bounded Euler coframe removes the apparent compact Wold smoothing. The valuation-one Route-T
current then equals a pure Wold half-density current plus a uniformly bounded trace-class
correction, and the two line-bound propositions are equivalent.

Ordered Abel summation splits the pure Wold current into an explicit logarithmic-mesh current, one
static first-prime holonomy atom, and a residual connection. The residual arithmetic primitive
tends to zero with an exponential square-root-log rate. Its square is summable against the exact
critical harmonic prime weight, so it is an unconditional finite-Fisher-energy vector; pointwise
damping is still too weak for total-variation closure. Independently, the unsquared theta
resolvent row and singular-metric two-port Green identity express the same current; theta-Green
plus adjacent-prime/Wold current cancels to a bounded dispersive background with an explicit
two-residue future carrier. Exterior reflection does not yield a second equation: reversing the
safe theta line and frequency negates the complete packet exactly and preserves its norm.

The physical observer is now classified. Its coefficient `p+1` is unbounded on the Fisher carrier,
so residual-only bounded same-carrier landing is impossible; nevertheless it is exactly the legal
lower contour shift by `-i/2`. The induced residual connection converges absolutely and is
`C^infinity` with every polynomial log moment. Negative-frequency polarization now extends it
holomorphically through the entire lower half-plane, and division by the coordinate gives a global
Hardy bound. The main logarithmic mesh is an exact right-endpoint quadrature whose
error reduces to the classical mean-square prime-gap sum.

This does not instantiate Route V. Lean now proves the exact two-sided mesh criterion: at critical
depth, absolute mesh-defect convergence is equivalent to the weighted consecutive-prime-gap
energy. That critical energy monotonically controls the whole complex mesh-defect series on
`-1/2 <= Re s <= 0`, including every shifted zero in `1/2 <= Re rho <= 1`; imaginary height changes
only a fixed prefactor. Formalizing the published mean-square prime-gap theorem would therefore
close the entire mesh half-strip input, but not Route T. Lean also proves the exact safe Laplace
symbol, holomorphy of every valuation
`>= 2` contribution on the critical half-plane, meromorphic continuation of the valuation-one
Wold carrier, and a nonzero residue at the translated location of every off-critical zeta zero;
later shifts and higher valuations cannot cancel it. Surviving scientific work is source-side
continuation/noncancellation of the complete prime-zeta-minus-mesh-minus-holonomy identity toward
`Re s=-1/2`, or the equivalent finite-stage source/divisor cancellation through the cofinal
limit. The reflected two-line scalar quadrature is closed and should not be pursued.

The former two-sided residual shortcut is closed negatively. Its critical endpoint graph norm
would force the same prime-gap energy while opening the residual half-strip, but item 51 proves
that combination impossible because it erases genuine critical Wold poles. The published
prime-gap import is therefore not optional on any surviving absolute-mesh lane.

A Lean-proved Abel-threshold module closes the local bridge from
prime-gap moments to this mesh. If the prefix gap-square moment grows like `p_M^theta` for any
`theta<3/2`, the critical energy is summable; the concrete `theta=11/8` specialization closes the
whole complex half-strip mesh. Thus the only literature-to-Lean debt in this component is the
published unconditional consecutive-prime-gap moment itself in the required prefix form. Neither
supplies the remaining Hardy/Wold noncancellation theorem.

### Tight-source coalitional / collective-repair architecture

The new universal-law theorems expose another possible producer grammar. Positive-carrier
attainment can be attacked by proving tightness or bounded proper pricing for finite source
coalitions, while semantically conservative repairs can create a positive circulation only through
an irreducibly collective threshold invisible to each repair separately. A new RH route would have
to define the arithmetic defects and repair interaction before observing zero locations, prove the
tightness/threshold law from the prime--Gamma source, and map the born circulation
coefficient-faithfully to an RH endpoint. None of these arithmetic objects exists in the corpus.
The cofinal viability layer adds a sharp promotion law: compact observer fibres and closed coherent
horizons make finite survival promote to one global source witness; failure forces every witness
out of every compact set and makes every predeclared proper source cost diverge. Compact legal
control fibres also make the endogenous predecessor commute with the cofinal intersection and
produce the greatest viable fixed law. This rules out false finite-horizon immortality as a
producer unless noncompact escape, nonclosed admission, or incoherent horizons are used. No
arithmetic observer, horizon, or Xi evaluation is instantiated. This is a route-design opening,
not Route V.

The new symbolic-execution capstone closes another false promotion. Executable grade-`m` repair
always implies the local failure inequalities, and for one failure kernel the converse holds. On
the three-kernel triangle, however, uniform weight `n` is locally admitted at grade `2n` but cannot
decompose into `2n` legal grade-one repairs; these nonexecutable grades are unbounded while the
normalized profile and parity observer stay constant. Thus local Hall/failure inequalities plus a
bounded normalized observer do not certify an executable arithmetic source law. Any route from
this opening must build the actual compatible decomposition/history, not only its symbolic cone.
No such arithmetic execution system or Xi handoff exists, so the theorem narrows the candidate
grammar rather than creating a route.

The primitive-execution refinement now gives the exact surviving invariant. Every positive
locally admitted repair has a canonical primitive decomposition; bounded grammar is equivalent to
bounded primitive degree. Triangle repair is generated in degree two, but the cofinal
complete-complement family has primitive degree `k-1`, so every bounded grammar misses an explicit
admitted law. Primitive degree spectrum is invariant under coordinate relabeling and under the
failure-antichain quotient. The exact primitive ray executes, its projective profile and cyclic
grade observer are periodic, yet its exact grade advances without bound. Thus a viable arithmetic
execution route needs a cofinal primitive spectrum or genuinely unbounded grammar; bounded
observer data cannot promote it. No prime--Gamma repair family or Xi handoff is instantiated, so
this is a sharper route-design opening, not a new route.

Positive observer transport preserves both symbolic admission and actual execution and therefore
cannot manufacture an execution gap. A noninjective triangle observer can nevertheless erase a
real source gap while creating false positive overlap. Observer collapse is therefore another
one-way information-loss mechanism, not a repair producer.

The expanded Nevanlinna corpus is likewise a classifier, not a producer. Canonical boundary leaves

sublinear moment growth is possible only through spectral collapse. Bounded source operations
