packet to one explicit absence-of-simultaneous-jet-telescoper premise; it does not prove that
premise. The exact Selberg-class carrier, continuation uniqueness, coefficientwise character
twists/depletion, multiple-standard-twist holomorphy, and concrete Artin character induction/
reciprocity layers enlarge the L-function platform. The Stark module proves only the finite integer
order classifier from its Artin--Takagi and Foote--Murty hypotheses; it does not derive those
analytic hypotheses for Artin L-functions. None has a Riemann-Xi source landing.

Finite reversible-chain additions prove the killed Dirichlet form, inverse/dual capacity,
equilibrium potential and Dirichlet principle; gate-word additions identify first-hit information
projection, energy--multiplicity decomposition, and the zero-fugacity minimal-length limit. The
frozen-admission resolvent-flow counterexample proves that positive moment flow and a positive
current canonical admission do not control the same source at a nearby frozen admission. These are
exact interfaces/no-gos for future E/U-style architectures, not new RH routes.
None supplies the source/mechanism/landing
triple required for Route V.

## 6. Fundamental nature of remaining problems

Across live routes, first unresolved statements fall into five mathematical classes.

| Class | Routes | Missing knowledge |
|---|---|---|
| singular source descent | C1, L, CCM-FU | positive closure or coefficient-faithful comparison before scalar quotient; L needs the global ideal-preserving completed-Green/BV lift into the fixed physical landing, while CCM-FU has closed finite-stage source completion and now needs canonical cofinal `Pi(lambda,k)` transport of four-unit incidence into full floor orientation |
| all-prime oscillatory transport | D2, E, T | cancellation or finite ceiling retaining arithmetic phase and physical normalization; E's unconditioned saddle coercivity and whole-prefix scalar cancellation are formally impossible. T has closed its exact-AFE fourth moment and detector exponent budget; it now needs the actual Ingham--Gabriel zero-counting interpolation, density-to-`7/10` prime supply, and signed source composition, or pole-preserving Wold continuation |
| adaptive spectral orientation | G, U | G needs source classification/lower control of its attained smooth-visible ground vector or completed physical forcing with the proved Hardy moment, global two-ray subtraction, physical zero-trace selection, and coupled all-aperture return. U's complete-divisor and common-filter prime--Gamma mixed flux both vanish unconditionally, leaving only source-specific diagonal classification for the fixed cofinal carrier |
| whole-space coercivity | O | classify the attained restricted-Weil ground direction and prove it is controlled by the explicit prolate/prime source; radial ODE tails and smooth visibility are already closed. N's analogous receiver has been terminalized |
| positive carrier / source-budget existence | P, Q | P needs a finite high-tail curvature ceiling or a completed primitive-Euler-to-Xi incidence/contraction; arbitrary-cluster positivity, exact spacing-free collision trace, and a primitive source budget below one strict-right zero quantum are closed. Q needs literal compact-measure/plateau formation after coupled pole--zeta--Gamma cancellation |

This explains repeated stalls. Classical functional analysis supplies receivers once sign,
boundedness, or coercivity is present. Corpus no-gos show these properties are not inherited from
ambient positivity, faithful re-encoding, local exactness, finite moments, bounded observer
towers, isometry, compact-carrier representation, or determinant packaging. The shared horizontal
carrier theorem sharpens this diagnosis: retained class and its orientation are already present;
missing theorem is an arithmetic law forcing its annihilation. More generally, decisive theorem
must act on source before completion removes reflection-odd, phase-sensitive, or adaptive boundary
coordinate. The exact longitudinal--transverse law sharpens “source selection” further: decisive
operation must mix zero-codiagonal source information into longitudinal divisor response; a
radical-preserving intervention can only rescale what was already there.

## 7. Retired mechanism ledger

The following constructions are closed at advertised strength:

- finite local selectors and bounded label-blind codiagonals;
- universal Gaussian/CCR/Fock positivity without source incidence;
- finite Hankel or determinant positivity presented as all-degree source positivity;
- fixed-width rectangular Weil floors;
- finite-height Sonin transfer and compact multiplicative repairs;
- one-angle entire Suzuki characteristic convergence;
- finite-index parity/Pontryagin comparison for Suzuki kernel;
- fixed finite Wold moments and polynomial seam patching as Möbius cancellation;
- Route-E arguments using only generalized-prime local decomposition, PNT/Mertens windows, or a
  finite ordinary-prime prefix;
- finite positive amplifier patterns as a uniform boundary bridge: their certified exclusion
  width is capped at inverse scale;
- scalar phase nodes and finite differential-algebraic prime-germ readers;
- unrenormalized Julia energy, post hoc diagonal subtraction, and bounded residual polarization;
- separable prime-depth incidence and vertex-gauge cycle holonomy;
- fixed clock-jet spectra, unordered scalar frequency fusion, and positive Gamma shorting;
- target-pulled metrics, ordinary Cartier exactness, and generic mapping cones as R/L producers;
- abstract cohomological duality, period-one Stokes normalization, scaling/Haar invariance, or
  reflection alone as a Route-R/L physical-coefficient selector;
- full vertical-gauge invariance as nonerasing descent: it annihilates the retained prime class,
  while choosing a gauge splitting leaves every coefficient possible;
- existence of an ordinary complex retaining Route-R raw curvature: universal stabilization
  supplies one at every grade, so naturality/minimality/admission must exclude it;
- Route R as an independent producer: intrinsic pre-polar annihilation is impossible, and every
  surviving positive-polar, compactness, singular-factorization, first-jet, or Fisher interface is
  RH-equivalent;
- closed/closable Calderón--Witt extensions or multivalued closed relations which agree with the
  old zero boundary on the dense arithmetic core;
- compact-orbit periodization or rotation-invariant cross-prime compact energy as Route-L
  producer: cofinal labels separate algebraically but compact topology erases noncompact escape;
- positive/passive completion of the centered `Lambda-1` Route-T current, and linear co-moving
  aperture extinction as a faithful mass detector;
- bounded same-carrier realization of Route T's physical `p+1` observer;
- reflected exterior theta packets as two independent Route-T quadratures: they are exact
  negatives with equal norm;
- one common Banach-algebra formation receiver carrying a bounded derivation with `D T=1`;
- algebraic prime augmentation as a new symplectic obstruction: reduction is only one hyperbolic
  plane and supplies no arithmetic Witt-rank producer;
- Hilbert or `c0` completion as a faithful all-prime one-port receiver: the augmentation constraint
  is dense and Hausdorff reduction erases the port; only the `ell1` phase preserves it. Finite
  phase recurrence and complete finite jet tomography do not repair this, and the global
  unweighted observer has no admitted infinitesimal generator;
- transverse complete-shell perturbations or radical-preserving interventions as a new divisor
  selector;
- first-order displacement tests of a complete functional-equation quadruple;
- new seed asymptotics or finite real-zero determinants as substitute for O whole-ground control;
- uniqueness/determinacy as substitute for P carrier existence;
- positive Gamma orientation, cumulant sign, fixed angular sector, or endpoint height as substitute
  for U adaptive crossing sign.

Every retired route remains retired unless new work changes primitive or defeats its exact no-go.

## 8. Literature exchange and novelty boundary

Primary literature supplies parent languages but not current Tier-1 source theorems.

- Hedenmalm's 2026 differential-pair spectral realization gives a new spectral interpretation of
  zeta zeros, but no T outgoing-current bound, N source-domain estimate, or U null-flag
  orientation: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17494
- Suzuki's 2026 screw-function formulation gives finite-interval self-adjoint operators and a
  conjectural limiting program, but no source-selected G transfer or U completed-source crossing
  sign: https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09096
- Berg--Szwarc characterize Hankel determinant/positivity subtleties; determinant nonnegativity
  does not supply PSD of every adaptive U face: https://arxiv.org/abs/1405.7267
- Salazar obtains total positivity from positive moment representations; this does not orient
  signed adaptive discrepancy after shifted-Xi factorial normalization:
  https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31647
- Matomäki--Radziwiłł--Tao's averaged Chowla/Elliott theorem and Menon's 2026 refinement give the
  relevant external correlation technology. Lean now proves the required small/large truncation
  bridge without a maximum over all endpoints. Remaining import is the exact uniform Möbius
  specialization, including nonpretentious hypotheses and rates, of the published theorem:
  https://arxiv.org/abs/1503.05121 and https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15574

Scoped searches found no exact external collision for T's complete mass-light-cone reduction or
U's Hilbert--charge/null-flag/Lorentz chain. This is evidence for corpus novelty, not proof of
historical priority. Specialist review remains required.

External results worth formalizing must be unconditional, source-specific, absent from corpus, and
must narrow first open premise. Additional abstract RH criteria, generic spectral models, or
moment theorems do not meet this bar.

## 9. Disproof frontier

One rigorously certified off-line Xi zero disproves RH. Equivalent finite negative witnesses are
already formalized through restricted-Weil floors, continued prime--archimedean tails, Hankel
faces, and several determinant criteria. No such witness exists in corpus.

Most explicit source-level disproof candidate is growing-order squared-Xi reverse-Bessel radial
jet. Orders through eighteen are positive; moving-prime comparison from order nineteen onward is
open. Numerical evidence is not accepted as proof or disproof.

## 10. Research priorities

### Shortest live chains

1. **P:** prove one of two explicit source budgets. Curvature lane: derive any finite uniform upper
   ceiling for the curvature-normalized positive tail on `|Im s|>233/20`; the central band is
   unconditional and the tail ceiling is exactly RH. Exterior lane: construct a completed,
   coefficient-faithful incidence/contraction from the primitive Euler collision carrier to the Xi-
   divisor collision carrier before divisor expansion. Lean already proves arbitrary-cluster PSD,
   the spacing-free zero trace, the P-G174 global primitive-source trace budget, and P-G175's
   strict inequality putting that entire budget below one zero quantum;
   neither clustering nor source collision summability remains open. A diagonal `N/4` budget still
   cannot supply the required carrier transport, but no receiver amplification is needed after it.
   Literal complex columns, non-diagonal overlap, pointed Xi reporter, Gram positivity, four-unit
   norm, projective Hardy rescaling, and cubic-kernel identity are closed. Full-frame Plancherel, generic contour
   norms, independent complement budgets, scalar Green regularization, and post-zero detector
   estimates do not supply either budget.
2. **L:** construct the source-derived singular comparison from completed Green `tensor` BV
   boundary flatness to the already fixed Burnol/Deninger physical landing before divisor
   specialization. It must commute with the resolved target differential, make the fixed physical
   reader annihilate the image boundary, and preserve multiplicity jets, ordinary-prime
   provenance, ideals, and both orientations. Strict resolved chain provenance already exists.
   Hazewinkel's printed pivot-division step is formally false in its stated coefficient algebra,
   but the separate monic Gröbner/projective-chart repair is now Lean-closed. No Witt/Hankel
   algebraic premise remains available to substitute for this RH-bearing comparison.
3. **T:** formalize/prove the actual Ingham--Gabriel coefficient-three interpolation and
   zero-counting theorem from the now-Lean-closed critical fourth moment, mollifier second moment,
   `4/3` detector-error moment, pole-regularized detector, and absorbed exponent `<7/10`. Then prove
   the density-to-short-interval consequence at exponent `7/10` and compose lower/upper phase,
   shell, layer, count, consecutive-prime-gap moment, and centered current. Exact AFE formation,
   primal/dual/pole budgets, unconditional `N^(101/100)` fourth moment, and detector exponent
   arithmetic are closed. Wold chart separately needs signed/coherent continuation retaining
   critical residues; repeated short-block freezing is dead.
4. **U:** prove a common-filter diagonal classification for the one fixed cofinal polynomial--
   Gaussian carrier: source-specifically lower-bound its even/detail diagonal capitals, or prove
   any negative diagonal forces a labelled prime--Gamma anomaly incompatible with the degree-free
   Cauchy density. Native complete-divisor exchange and the exact common-filter prime--Gamma mixed
   source flux both tend to zero unconditionally. The Nyquist successor is therefore retired;
   universal diagonal positivity is RH-equivalent and cannot be assumed.
5. **E:** prove finite physical weighted projection
   `d_E^2 <= X^(-1+o(1))`, equivalently
   `N * Var_(nu/N)(rho/nu) <= X^(o(1))` or all-reporter minimum-neighbor expansion, then
   normalized seam flux and recurrence accumulation for fixed
   entropy-complement relation. Finite mixture, corrected load threshold, parity, and endpoint
   consumer are formal. Exact normalized weighted Hall is zero-energy stronger route; one-sided
   `1+o(1)` load is rate-insufficient. Universal Fisher contraction is rate-excluded as producer:
   forced
   shell clock is logarithmic and gauge-neutralized energy grows. Separately formalize the
   published averaged-Chowla theorem
   with the exact Möbius specialization, or prove the direct Gallagher lane's now-isolated
   `OffDiagonalSmall mu hh` premise. Its fixed/cofinally low-height and rough parts are closed; the analytic
   target is the coprime Möbius near-diagonal covariance hidden inside the Ramaré off-diagonal in
   the moving range `delta_M^(-2/3) << T << M`. The new ternary-marker mean-square theorem handles
   the finite marker cube unconditionally in Lean, including exact alternating coefficients,
   reciprocal Gibbs weights, cofinal antiscale saturation, canonical vanishing quadratic mass, and
   the actual coprime Möbius-product bridge. Statewise rough-cofactor flatness has now been reduced
   to one centered diffuse codiagonal reporter, and its exact physical energy is weighted
   marginal-projection distance. Prove cofactor tensoring plus all-reporter rate or
   recurrence-compatible collision defect, with same moving seam. Maximum-state collision and mean
   Euler component are already bounded; prime spacing, finite marker dissipation, square-multiple
   control, universal Fisher corner identification, critical-clock parameter choice, and
   strict-right graph normalization are not live gaps.
   Alternate direct lane: instantiate grown Lean conductance on actual recurrence kernel `P` and
   prove weighted-isolated mass `<=X^(1/2+o(1))` and inverse row-plus-column-load Dirichlet
   energy `E_P<=X^(o(1))` on full physical entropy-complement graph. One-sign restriction gives
   co-moving reverse chi-square, equivalent at strict-half scale, so retain parity-agnostic rows
   and prove common-aperture closure.
   Do not pursue unconditioned scalar saddle coercivity: its fixed-power form is formally false at
   the explicit antiscale resonance. Shell conditioning or the fixed grade-recurrent relation is
   mandatory.
   Co-moving contribution already has correct size; symmetrization needs no Metropolis overlap or
   detailed balance. Biregular block estimates and bounded-prime local replacement graph are
   fenced.
6. **C1/CCM-FU:** prove a source-derived real-spectrum/positive-closure theorem or
   intersection/Ward incidence. Determinant, essential-trace, weighted-derivative,
   shifted-cokernel, and supersymmetric capstones are already certified. Extend the
   now-Lean-closed `m = 2` operator residue law (relative one-step and double-defect estimates hold
   for every `m >= 2`) to `m >= 3` via the k-fold defect chains, then prove the global trace identity
   that forces the already-positive Gauss--Hodge mass to vanish and identify it with the actual
   Weil/intersection object. The pointwise mass gap inside `xiWeilPolarization`, virial
   normalization, all-prime summability classification, GNS socket, Krein signature decomposition,
   and generic inverse-response Ward law are formal. Direct reflection-pair square completion is
   terminal because its negative sector is exactly the Hodge mass; missing producer is the
   arithmetic compressed-trace/anomalous-Ward identity. The unconditional Weil cone and probe-
   circle chart formally show that positive ray-jet budgets and finite holomorphic sampling cannot
   orient the Hermitian defect. Perform
   widening-limit assembly only for parallel connector branches.
7. **O:** classify the attained smooth-visible restricted-Weil ground vector and prove a literal
   source-side subexponential upper bound for its negative floor. Profile exhaustion/bulk coercivity
   must compare this true ground direction to the explicit prolate/prime family. Actual-PSWF
   leading-density error, inverse-bandwidth separation, and whole-channel assembly remain seed-
   level inputs; ODE decay and per-channel polar summability are closed. Completed Suzuki/Evans and
   noncompact incidence/direct-floor producers remain independent.
8. **G:** settle one of two literal source branches. Floor branch: identify the source-selected
   attained ground vector and prove lower control/profile exhaustion for the complete restricted-
   Weil family; smooth density and equality of Rayleigh infima are already closed. Exterior branch:
   construct the completed physical forcing with the first-moment bound consumed by the new Hardy
   theorem, perform global two-ray subtraction/support reduction, and prove physical zero-trace and
   coupled all-aperture return. Homogeneous Fourier-profile globalization, strip propagation, and
   one-prime terminal propagator are closed.
9. **D2:** obtain a literal high-frequency ceiling for the continued prime--Gamma source. Route N
   is a terminalized receiver, not a parallel live task.
10. **Q:** prove direct Cassini--Jensen/jet saturation. Q's selected continuous power-reciprocal
    carrier is retired; any reuse requires a source-derived discontinuous plateau quotient.

### Work unlikely to move frontier

- more RH-equivalent scalar coordinates;
- finite-prefix positivity;
- generic compactness without source admission;
- more Route O seed asymptotics without ground-space classification;
- more Route E moments or seam patch algebra without unit physical incidence;
- more Route-E/GHS mean-value, fibre-kernel, or maximal-iteration packaging without either the
  published averaged-Chowla premise, `OffDiagonalSmall mu hh`, or rate-sharp physical
  weighted-projection/all-reporter law;
- more fixed-height/PNT-Abel or rough-number estimates for Route E without entering the remaining
  prime off-diagonal Ramaré/Halász range;
- more full-allocation whole-anchor transport: global terminal capacity is formally insufficient;
- more Route R frames, corona quotients, compactness, singular Xi-factorization, first-jet, or
  Fisher-landings as independent producers: the terminal classifier proves every admitted branch
  RH-equivalent;
- more Route U local contact signs, projective monotonicity, reciprocal clearing, raw clock capital,
  or generic determinant lines without excluding fixed-carrier cofinal source inversion;
- more Route T AFE decomposition, local amplitude estimates, contour bookkeeping, cross-term
  pricing, or fourth-moment repackaging: exact three-source budgeting and the required fourth
  moment are closed; only the actual zero-density interpolation, `7/10` supply, and signed route
  composition move the arithmetic chart;
- formalization work on conjectures whose mathematical source theorem is not proved.

### L and R

All L/R anchors listed here are Lean-proved at this cutoff. In particular the chain-lift
obstruction, Green-sum range theorem, finite depletion tower, converse, exact-kernel,
Euler-depleted-kernel, Meyer-topology, and continuous-moment files receive formal credit.

## 13. Current frontier conclusion

At committed cutoff `bb1411afe4443137baf28d38521ab418d4d3a59f`, with the explicitly audited
post-cutoff shared-tree declarations above, RH and its negation remain
unproved. Live independent lettered routes are C1, D2, E, G, L, O, P, Q, T, and U, with CCM-FU as
a live unlettered lane. Route R is terminalized as an independent producer. No Route V is
instantiated by the triptic, Nevanlinna, formation, Suzuki-carrier, Ward, or prime-log jump--Sonin
infrastructure.

Current first unresolved theorems are:

- E cannot use unconditioned scalar saddle coercivity or odd-grade determinant cancellation: Lean
  proves the fixed-power comparison eventually fails at the explicit antiscale resonance, Gamma
  completion is nonattenuating, the terminal chamber makes all odd grades reinforce, and the whole
  critical prime prefix has negative linear-grade asymptotic `-2sqrt(X)/log X` with nonvanishing
  Euler-prefix modulus at Mertens scale; the exact variance fixes physical shell width. It must retain
  that shell before scalarization
  or replace the capacity-impossible whole-anchor allocation with structured truncation, growing
  arity, many-to-many incidence, or pre-column cancellation, then prove the fixed relation's
  strict-half Hall/flux/cost theorem. Injective-parameter multi-node separation does not supply
  capacity.
- G has two internal branches: source-derived classification/lower control of the attained
  smooth-visible restricted-Weil ground vector, or completed physical forcing plus coupled all-
  aperture exterior source return. Whole-frequency Gamma assembly, the terminal-`L2` trace classifier, finite
  delta-derivative point-support structure, principal Gamma no-atom law, and the no-point-support
  theorem for the full weak Gamma equation are closed. Half-line distributional classification is
  also closed with one vector on each whole ray. Homogeneous upper-strip gluing is classified
  exactly by `c_-=-i*c_+`, equivalently zero half-log endpoint trace, and the glued solution is
  square-integrable on every interior strip line. A single Fourier profile now represents every
  interior height, and the forced-ray Hardy theorem reduces `L2` propagation to a first-moment
  bound on the forcing. Exterior path now needs the completed physical forcing with that moment,
  global two-ray subtraction, support reduction of the residual to the full equation, and a source
  theorem forcing the physical trace relation with all-aperture orientation. Rooted prime signs,
  centered descendant algebra, exact support cutoff, wall-commutator bounds, and one-prime terminal
  propagator are closed; forcing formation/trace selection are not. Flat terminal charge and
  bounded-dipole matching are automatic. Suzuki natural-product
  symmetry, the complex mixed source form, and its exact equality with the compressed Suzuki screw
  and actual Xi-zero Weil distribution are closed. CCM-FU's parity-free finite
  simple-ground Fourier theorem, fixed-support mean-square compact-Fourier zero transfer, and local
  field-one/Suzuki four-half-step factorization are closed. It must construct canonical
  `Pi(lambda,k)`, prove its block/simple-ground hypotheses cofinally, prove the canonical arithmetic
  sources satisfy the transfer theorem with nonzero Xi limit, and orient its four-unit incidence in
  the complete ground selector. Its exact finite `mu(n)/n`
  seam to Route E is a new opening, but lacks a cofinal
  Hall/load/flux-to-floor comparison and is not yet another route.
- L must construct the global strict ideal-preserving completed-Green/BV lift into the fixed
  Burnol/Deninger physical reader. Hazewinkel saturation, Floquet resonance quotient and bounded-
  correction no-go, and Milnor residue/Gersten plumbing are closed. Garland torsion is adjacent
  classical formalization backlog, not the RH-bearing square.
- O must prove the actual-PSWF-to-leading-density error, inverse-bandwidth estimate, and physical
  whole-channel assembly that instantiate its closed arithmetic diagonal/projective trace-limit
  chain, then compare the canonically selected, quantitatively gapped constrained Hermite ladder
  with the attained true restricted-Weil ground vector. ODE radial decay, channel square
  summability, per-channel polar control, ground attainment, and smooth-source visibility are
  closed.
- P must prove either a finite uniform ceiling for the positive high-frequency curvature-normalized
  tail or a completed coefficient-faithful incidence/contraction from primitive Euler collision
  cells to Xi-divisor collision space. Arbitrary zero clusters are PSD; their trace is spacing-free;

  P-G175 proves that entire budget is strictly below the least charge of one strict-right zero.
  The former diagonal `N/4` budget is still insufficient because it supplies no carrier transport,
  but a completed trace contraction now closes RH without density amplification.
  Scalar contractivity, exact scalar unitary completion, differentiable unit-frame overlap, and
  multiplicative Gamma-sewn recovery are formally insufficient or RH-equivalent.
- T has closed exact literal primal, dual, and confluent-pole budgets and, from exact AFE equality,

in one member of a fixed countable critical-feature family, uniformly in height and aperture. This
removes height escape and reporter degeneration from the singular five-shift landing problem.

P-G178 identifies a nontrivial arithmetic block for that survivor. On primitive cells set

```text
D(p,e)=(p,2e+1),  n=p^(e+1),  g(p,e)=sqrt n.
```

Lean proves `valuation(D pe)=n^2`, exact log-clock doubling, and reflected-incidence transport
`a -> 2a`; hence P-G173's tagged doubled Hardy node is the actual arithmetic squared clock. Gain
`g` exactly preserves resolved von Mangoldt half-density but is unbounded on the prime-two depth
tower, ruling out bounded counting-norm reindexing. Strongest survivor is positive:

```text
w(D pe) g(pe)^2 = w(pe)/8,
sum_(pe in S) w(D pe)|g(pe)f(pe)|^2
  =(1/8) sum_(pe in S) w(pe)|f(pe)|^2.                 (P-G178.1)
```

Thus cubic moment is precisely compatible with dyadic clock dilation: it converts unbounded
coefficient-preserving modular gain into an exact strict weighted contraction. This is a literal
pre-zero source incidence, not the completed source-to-divisor landing. Exact remaining seam is to
carry this depth-square block through a source-defined Gamma--Euler/Xi conormal or determinant-line
continuation and prove its polar cubic form is P-G173, without defining the map from zeros or
importing P-G176 capacity.

P-G179 proves that P-G178's weight algebra is geometric. For P-G177 feature columns `phi_pe`, Lean
proves the pointwise and finite-synthesis intertwiner

```text
g(pe) phi_(D pe)(r)=(1/2)phi_pe(2r),
integral_0^infinity |(1/2)f(2r)|^2dr
  =(1/8)integral_0^infinity |f(r)|^2dr.                (P-G179.1)
```

The exact source contraction is therefore ordinary physical half-line dilation after Hankel
synthesis. Remaining landing must preserve this arithmetic/physical intertwiner through
Gamma--Euler continuation; an unrelated abstract contraction is no substitute.

P-G180 closes the internal dyadic classification. If

```text
psi_pe(r)=q_pe r exp(-lambda_pe r),  Vf(r)=f(2r)/2,
```

then Lean proves `g(pe)psi_(D pe)=Vpsi_pe` and `phi_pe=(I-V)psi_pe`. Moreover

```text
sum_(k<N)V^k phi_pe=psi_pe-V^N psi_pe,
||V^N f||_2^2=8^(-N)||f||_2^2 -> 0.                   (P-G180.1)
```

Thus doubled-node difference is complete Calderón/Wold innovation data and `(I-V)` reconstructs
the base column. It cannot by itself create singular divisor curvature. The next attack must
conjugate through this explicit reconstruction and continue the base Gamma--Euler covariance;
further dyadic defects or abstract contraction estimates are retired.

P-G181 proves that this conjugation preserves the exact separation threshold. The one-scale base
Cauchy cube is a principal submatrix of P-G173's doubled Schur cube, hence PSD, and has diagonal

```text
B_R(a,a,0)=1/(4a^3).                                   (P-G181.1)
```

On primitive Euler cells its weight and total budget are exactly

```text
w_base=(216/115)w_collision,
B_base=(216/115)B_collision<2.                         (P-G181.2)
```

Every strict-right zero has `0<a<=1/2`, hence costs at least `2`. Lean proves the universal base
trace-capacity law is equivalent to RH. This does not prove RH, but removes the entire four-term
dyadic defect from the missing producer: it is enough to land the coefficient-faithful completed
base covariance on the Xi divisor before zero expansion.

P-G182 identifies that target with an actual completed object. For

```text
C(z,r)=logDeriv(xi)(z) r exp(-(z-1/2)r),
```

the multiplicity-normalized residue at a Xi zero `rho` is
`psi_rho(r)=r exp(-(rho-1/2)r)`, and Lean proves

```text
integral_(0,infinity) conj(psi_rho(r)) psi_tau(r) dr
  =2/[conj(rho-1/2)+(tau-1/2)]^3.                     (P-G182.1)
```

Thus every finite normalized conormal residue Gram equals P-G181's base Cauchy matrix exactly.
The raw logarithmic residue nevertheless carries multiplicity `m`, so its energy is quadratic in
`m`; the divisor target is linear. Lean proves equality occurs iff `m=1`, and the unique positive
scalar correction is `1/sqrt(m)`. The remaining theorem is consequently not identification of a
zero-side kernel but construction and pre-divisor norm control of a coefficient-faithful
half-density conormal landing (equivalently, canonical splitting over the analytic-order copy
fiber). Assuming simple zeros or normalizing after observing target capacity would be circular.

P-G183 proves that the needed multiplicity split is already canonical. If `m` is analytic order,
the equal split of a total residue `v` over the existing Xi copy fibre is

```text
Split_m(v)_j=v/m,
sum_j Split_m(v)_j=v,
sum_j |Split_m(v)_j|^2=|v|^2/m.                       (P-G183.1)
```

For any copy vector `u` conserving the raw residue `m psi`, Lean proves the exact variance law

```text
sum_j |u_j|^2=m|psi|^2+sum_j |u_j-psi|^2.             (P-G183.2)
```

Thus equal splitting is the unique minimum-energy lift, its operator norm is `1/sqrt(m)`, and
applying it to P-G182's actual raw completed-Xi conormal residue gives one base radial feature on
every multiplicity copy. Multiplicity is no longer open. The sole Route P producer is now the
support-level coefficient-faithful continuation from primitive base synthesis to the raw
completed-Xi conormal current, with a pre-residue norm bound; copy resolution can only decrease
energy.

### Route T exact dual carrier: Gaussian tail and height variation close

T-C8e-E closes the two analytic debts left by T-C8e-C/D.  The global off-pole digamma argument gives
an unconditional polynomial envelope

```text
|d/dtau [chi(s_tau-(1+iv))/chi(s_tau)]|
  <= D (1+|tau|)^2 (1+|v|)^2,
```

while the central neutral-slope estimate retains its sharper relative `1/tau` factor.  Splitting at
`2|v|=tau` and only then taking norms yields one height-independent majorant for every `tau>=4` and
every smoothing scale `Y>0`:

```text
|dualHeightDerivativeIntegrand(tau,Y,v)|
  <= (K/Y) (1+|v|)^4 exp(-v^2).
```

The point is structural: on the central range, chi-ratio size `O(tau(1+|v|))` cancels the neutral
logarithmic slope `O((1+|v|)/tau)`; on the outer range, `tau<2|v|` transfers the global height
polynomial into the Gaussian frequency polynomial.  The apparent large reflected tail was therefore
a shadow of demanding one estimate before separating neutral and non-neutral regimes.

`SmoothAFEZetaDualWeightHeightDerivative.lean` proves continuity and integrability of the exact
derivative integrand and differentiates the full Gaussian contour under the integral sign.
`SmoothAFEZetaDualWeightDerivativeBound.lean` proves the quartic-Gaussian majorant and the stronger
height-free law

```text
|d/dtau smoothAFEZetaDualWeight(tau,Y)| <= A/Y,

|smoothAFEZetaDualWeight(tau,Y)-smoothAFEZetaDualWeight(u,Y)|
  <= (A/Y)|tau-u|                         (tau,u>=4).
```

Both modules are Lean-proved. This proves **T-C8e-E (global Gaussian derivative exchange)** and
**T-C8e-F (reciprocal-scale exact-carrier freezing)**. `SmoothAFEZetaDualSeriesFreezing.lean` then
performs the freezing after summing the complete reflected Dirichlet series: exact critical
monomials have norm `(n+1)^(-1/2)`, both weighted series are summable, and

```text
norm(dualSeries(tau,tau,x)-dualSeries(tau,u,x))
  <= (A/x)|tau-u| smoothAFEZetaMajorant(3/2).
```

No Dirichlet phase is frozen or bounded termwise. Gaussian-frequency tail control, exact dual-
weight height variation, and full reflected dual-series freezing are therefore closed. Remaining
single-carrier debt passes to the squared neutral-carrier law below. The eventual fourth-moment
argument must retain Dirichlet oscillation; coefficient-three zero density, `7/10` prime supply,
and route composition follow only after that step.

### Route T infinite affine transfer and squared neutral-carrier law

T-C8e-G first tests and rejects fixed positive contour shift as the primary cure for the reflected
tail.  Any fixed pointwise power still leaves the transition block with square-root-scale absolute
mass.  The obstruction is not lack of decay; it is loss of Dirichlet oscillation.  The correct
pivot is an exact finite-to-infinite Montgomery--Vaughan transfer.  For every coefficient sequence
with absolute mass, square mass, and `n`-weighted square mass summable,

```text
integral[-T,T] |L(a,sigma+iy)|^2 dy
  <= 5*pi*e * sum_n n |a_n n^-sigma|^2
     + (5*pi/2) T * sum_n |a_n n^-sigma|^2.
```

`GeneralAffineMeanSquareInfinite.lean` proves this by arbitrary finite truncation, uniform
convergence of the exact L-series on the interval, dominated integral convergence, and monotone
comparison of finite square masses with their `tsum`s.  Thus the exact infinite reflected carrier
can be priced by oscillation without a truncation remainder.  This closes the support-limit debt;
it does not by itself supply the zeta-square AFE.

T-C8e-H identifies the missing zeta-square carrier.  Its normalized archimedean factor is not the
single ratio but

```text
R_tau(v)^2 = [chi(s_tau-(1+iv))/chi(s_tau)]^2.
```

Naive global product bounds suggest three height powers in its derivative.  Central relative
variation plus the outer implication `tau < 2|v|` instead proves the corpus-new law

```text
|d/dtau R_tau(v)^2| <= K (1+tau)(1+|v|)^5,       tau>=4.       (T-C8e-H.1)
```

The corresponding exact Gaussian square-dual weight

```text
W2_tau(Y)=(1/(2*pi)) integral_v R_tau(v)^2 M_Y(1+iv) dv
```

is differentiable under the integral and satisfies

```text
|d/dtau W2_tau(Y)| <= A(1+tau)/Y,                           (T-C8e-H.2)
|W2_tau(Y)-W2_u(Y)| <= A(1+2T)|tau-u|/Y                    (T-C8e-H.3)
```

for `tau,u in [T,2T]`, `T>=4`.  At the natural square-AFE scale `Y=(n+1)x`, `x` comparable to
`T`, this is the exact coefficientwise `1/(n+1)` gain.  Multiplication by critical amplitude
`(n+1)^(-1/2)` and divisor coefficient `d(n+1)` gives the genuinely summable mass

```text
sum_n d(n+1)/(n+1)^(3/2) < infinity.                         (T-C8e-H.4)
```

Consequently the complete square-dual divisor-series weight can be frozen on a dyadic block with
error

```text
<= [A(1+2T)/x] |tau-u| sum_n d(n+1)/(n+1)^(3/2),             (T-C8e-H.5)
```

without freezing or taking absolute values of the critical Dirichlet phases.  The underlying
universal principle is **archimedean charge neutrality at balanced conductor scale**: logarithmic
height differentiation removes one conductor power from the squared normalized carrier, and the
balanced square-AFE smoothing scale removes the remaining power.  Earlier tail failures were
shadows of estimating the charged carrier before this normalization and scale cancellation.

`SmoothAFEZetaChiShiftSquareDerivativeBound.lean`,
`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualWeightHeightDerivative.lean`,
`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualWeightDerivativeBound.lean`, and
`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualSeriesFreezing.lean` prove these statements unconditionally.
The weight itself also satisfies

```text
|W2_tau(Y)| <= K(1+|tau|)^2/Y,
```

so one reciprocal smoothing scale is present before height differencing. This closes absolute
square-dual size control; it does not provide the required lower/two-sided localization of the
full derivative series.

`Sieve/SmoothAFEZetaSquarePoleDecomposition.lean` constructs the literal Gaussian zeta-square
Mellin integrand, rewrites it as a pole-filled quotient, evaluates its simple
coefficient, differentiates the pole-filled numerator at the shifted double pole, computes the
confluent residue coefficient, and gives the exact simple-pole/double-pole/remainder decomposition.
`Sieve/SmoothAFEZetaSquareVerticalIntegrability.lean` proves Gaussian control on the right line and
quartic-Gaussian control on the reflected left line. `SmoothAFEZetaSquareHorizontalDecay.lean`
proves both horizontal integrals vanish. `SmoothAFEZetaSquareContour.lean` gives the exact finite
rectangle residue crossing, and `SmoothAFEZetaSquareInfiniteContour.lean` passes to the infinite
line identity. `SmoothAFEZetaSquareRightExpansion.lean` justifies termwise integration and
identifies the normalized right boundary integral with the exact divisor-weighted Gaussian cutoff
series. `SmoothAFEZetaSquareLeftExpansion.lean` applies the functional equation on the reflected
line, proves a summable integral majorant for the full dual divisor series, and identifies the
normalized left boundary with the negative dual-weight series. Finally,
`SmoothAFEZetaSquareExactAFE.lean` combines both expansions and the confluent residue law into the
exact normalized Gaussian AFE for `zeta(1/2+i*tau)^2`, including the explicit shifted double-pole
correction. Gaussian source formation, local pole algebra, global contour crossing, both boundary
expansions, and symmetric AFE assembly are closed.

`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualNegativeContour.lean` now shifts the square-dual smoothing
weight itself across its residue at `w=0`. It proves analyticity on `Re w>-1/2`, exact finite and
infinite rectangular contour identities, integrability on `Re w=-1/4`, and vanishing of both
horizontal edges. The resulting source identity is

~~~
smoothAFEZetaSquareDualWeight(tau,Y)
  = 1 + smoothAFEZetaSquareDualWeightAt(-1/4,tau,Y).
~~~

This extracts the neutral constant exactly and provides a literal negative-contour carrier. The
constant residue is independent of height, so differentiating the shifted identity removes it
exactly rather than estimating it.

`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualQuarterHeightDerivative.lean` proves exact height derivatives for the
quarter-line chi-shift ratio and its square. On `tau>=4` and `2|v|<=tau`, it controls the
corresponding log-derivative increment by

~~~
(16/tau^2 + 2*pi/tau) * (1+|v|).
~~~

`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualQuarterGlobalSlope.lean` proves a uniform linear digamma envelope on the
positive eighth strip containing all four quarter-contour arguments. On the complementary range
`tau<2|v|`, conductor powers are therefore converted into powers of `1+|v|` and absorbed by the
Gaussian. `SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualQuarterDerivativeBound.lean` combines both ranges into the
corpus-new pointwise law

~~~
|d/dtau R_tau,-1/4(v)^2|
  <= C (1+|v|)^5 / [tau (tau^(1/4))^2],              tau>=4.
~~~

Thus the negative-quarter carrier derivative has the exact `tau^(-3/2)` scale. The two explicit
quarter powers are retained in Lean, avoiding an unnecessary real-power normalization.

`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualQuarterGlobalDerivative.lean` separately proves the all-height polynomial
majorant and frequency continuity needed only for dominated differentiation.
`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualNegativeContourDerivative.lean` differentiates the negative-quarter
integral and then differentiates the residue identity. Lean therefore proves that the original
square-dual weight has exactly the negative-contour derivative; no residue error survives.
Gaussian integration in `SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualSmallScaleDerivativeBound.lean` gives

~~~
|d/dtau W2_tau(Y)|
  <= A Y^(1/4) / [tau (tau^(1/4))^2].                (T-C8e-I.1)
~~~

Combining this with the positive-contour law `A(1+tau)/Y`,
`SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualTwoSidedDerivativeBound.lean` proves the exact two-sided localization

~~~
|d/dtau W2_tau(Y)|
  <= min(C Y^(1/4)/[tau (tau^(1/4))^2], C(1+tau)/Y). (T-C8e-I.2)
~~~

The crossover is `Y~tau^2`; at `Y=(n+1)x` with `x~tau`, this is precisely divisor-index
localization at `n~tau`. `SmoothAFEZetaSquareDualTwoSidedVariation.lean` integrates the same law on
dyadic height blocks without freezing any Dirichlet phase.

The higher principle is **constant-residue derivative localization**: a contour shift may be poor
for the carrier itself but optimal after height differentiation whenever every crossed residue is
height-neutral. Earlier failure to obtain a lower-conductor derivative was a shadow of estimating
before subtracting the neutral residue.

`Sieve/DuhamelMeanSquare.lean` now proves the oscillation-preserving transport inequality

~~~
integral_a^b |integral_a^t F(r,t) dr|^2 dt
  <= (b-a) integral_a^b integral_a^b |F(r,t)|^2 dt dr.       (T-C8e-I.3)
~~~

This is the correct replacement for absolute short-block freezing: at each derivative height `r`,
the full Dirichlet phase in `t` remains available to the infinite affine mean-square theorem.

Exact Gaussian AFE formation, negative-contour differentiation, two-sided square-conductor
localization, and abstract Duhamel transfer are closed. The coefficient-mass and affine theorems
below close the dual arithmetic input; the subsequent three-summand budget theorem closes the
required `T^(101/100)` fourth moment. RH remains open at the zero-density interpolation after that
moment theorem.

### T-C8e-J: contour-complementary derivative masses and phase-faithful affine landing

The two contour branches now combine at coefficient level instead of being treated as competing
global estimates.  For the exact centered derivative coefficient

a_n(T,r,t)=d(n+1)(n+1)^(-1/2+it)

|a_n| <= K(1+r)T^(-1)(n+1)^(-599/400),
|a_n| <= K T^(-5/4)(n+1)^(-99/400)       (T<=r).

On `r in [N,2N]`, the negative-quarter branch controls `n<N` and the positive-contour branch

sum_n |a_n(N,r,t)|^2       <= A N^(-399/200),

complementarity as Littlewood--Paley splitting**: the residue-neutral negative contour is the

critical mass law.

and the pointwise inequality

It follows in Lean that

```text
integral_[N,2N] |zeta(1/2+it)|^4 <= C N^(101/100).
```

The old demand for signed cross-term control was unnecessary: exact AFE equality plus the
three-term Hilbert-space inequality closes the required upper bound without estimating cross
terms. `SmoothAFEZetaSquareInghamCriticalMoment.lean` then identifies this integral with the
literal critical fourth moment used by Ingham's mollified detector, proves the mollifier second
moment with exponent `1001/1000`, derives the detector-error `4/3` moment
`O(N^(251/250))`, and verifies

```text
inghamRouteTHeightExponent(3/250,1/1000) < 7/10.
```

Route T is therefore no longer blocked on AFE formation, primal/dual/pole pricing, cross-term
assembly, the critical fourth moment, mollifier loss, or exponent bookkeeping. Its first open
scientific theorem is the actual Ingham--Gabriel interpolation and zero-counting argument that
turns these formal moments and the existing pole-regularized detector into the coefficient-three
zero-density estimate. After that come the density-to-short-interval prime theorem at exponent
`7/10` and the already-described source-faithful composition into the centered Route-T current.

### Route T fixed-right-edge correction and power-saving endpoint

`InghamRightEdgeMeanSquare.lean` first proves the literal cutoff-uniform estimate

```text
integral_[-T,T] |zeta(beta+it) M_X(beta+it)-1|^2 dt <= C_beta T  (beta>1).
```

Heavy-conjecture audit then falsifies this as a sufficient Gabriel input: uniform `O(T)` throws
away the exact coefficient gap below `X` and cannot produce coefficient three. The positive
survivor in `InghamRightEdgePowerSaving.lean` fixes `beta=181/180`, retains
`a_X(n)=0` for `n<=X`, and combines `d(n)<=C n^(1/360)` with exact p-series tails. Lean proves

```text
sum_n n |a_X(n)n^(-181/180)|^2 <= C^2 X^(-1/180)/(1/180),
sum_n   |a_X(n)n^(-181/180)|^2 <= C^2 X^(-181/180)/(181/180),
integral_[-X,X] |zeta(181/180+it) M_X(181/180+it)-1|^2 dt
  <= C_right X^(-1/180).
```

All three statements are unconditional and axiom-clean modulo Lean's standard quotient/choice
principles. This supplies genuine right-endpoint decay, not merely bounded normalized average.
At the fixed geometry `(alpha,sigma,beta)=(1/2,7/10,181/180)`, it leaves positive room after the
formal critical `251/250` loss. Exact remaining first obstruction is now Gabriel's integral-mean
interpolation itself, then the already-formal detector's quantitative boundary-to-zero-count
assembly. RH remains open.

### Route T concrete Gabriel-family advance

Five new axiom-clean modules now close the exact finite-dimensional and pole geometry behind the
coefficient-three interpolation:

- `InghamFixedGabrielScalarCore.lean`: exact Hadamard cancellation and `237/364` center exponent;
- `InghamFixedGabrielDualPower.lean`: analytic dual multiplier `q(z)=(1+180z)/127` with exact
  left-fourth/right-square identities;
- `InghamFixedGabrielScalarFamily.lean`: literal truncated scalar family and exact center-moment
  representation;
- `InghamGabrielPoleClearedError.lean`: Gaussian residue-unit error analytic across `z=1`, exact
  raw-error identity, vertical Gaussian decay, contractive pole factor on `Re z>=1/2`, and reverse
  center transfer with cost `(7/3)e^kappa`;
- `InghamDetectorLogMoment.lean`: literal detector logarithm bounded by the resulting
  `364/237` moment.

This is frontier progress, but not yet a zero-density theorem. Current first open theorem is the
sharp endpoint Hölder/boundedness audit for the concrete Gaussian scalar integral. Once closed,
the existing scalar Hadamard core yields the interior moment with formal exponent
`8263/11850 = 7/10-16/5925`; detector zero counting and density-to-prime supply remain after it.
